PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [103] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双分支框架DualMLC，用自回归解码器语言模型与双向编码器独立评分后晚融合，在大规模多标签文本分类三个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多标签文本分类需从数千至数万候选标签中选出少量相关标签；虽预训练语言模型提升了语义表示，但现有方法多依赖单一主编码器或在单一排序器内合并辅助特征，异构语言模型之间的互补性未被充分挖掘。

Method: DualMLC采用双分支框架：同一文档分别输入自回归解码器语言模型和双向编码器，每个分支保留独立表示通路，并在共享标签空间上独立估计相关性分数；随后通过晚logit融合（late logit fusion）合并两个得分向量。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的大规模多标签文本分类基准上达到SOTA；消融实验证实异构预测器集成产生的排序优于任一单分支。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型的互补证据（共享证据强化相关标签，分支特有证据互相补偿表示局限）能显著提升多标签分类排序性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [104] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文考察梵语在子词分词下的信息密度是否保留。基于相同FLORES-200内容，梵语在已部署分词器下消耗英语1.774–2.187倍、印地语1.325–1.353倍的token。与匹配的英语对照组相比，梵语"更省token"的优势消失。比率可分解为字符长度比与每字符token数比，后者接近1，真正存活的是字符级长度。结论落脚于部署实践：在当代散文与FLORES上，梵语每命题消耗英语1.831–2.899个token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语通过词尾融合格、数、人称与时态，并将小句连锁为复合词，因而每词信息密度高。然而这种密度能否在子词分词后存活尚属未知，且应以"每单位意义"而非"每词"为单位来度量。论文旨在检验：梵语的信息密度优势在分词化后是否仍是分词器或语料带来的假象。

Method: 在相同FLORES-200 devtest内容上比较token开销；用BPE在梵语侧与英语侧分别训练匹配的分词器（相同算法、相同语料、相同词表规模32k/64k/128k），设置8个尺寸匹配的配对，每臂对照配对匹配与字节匹配两种控制；将比率分解为字符长度比与每字符token数比；并测试当代散文、梵语韵文及SLP1转写等条件。

Result: 梵语在部署分词器（词表≥200,019）下消耗英语1.774–2.187倍token，仅为印地语token的1.325–1.353倍。对已部署英语分词器，梵语训练的BPE臂在当代散文上看似每命题更便宜（0.887），但匹配控制下该优势消失：32k与64k时8个配对全部>1.0且95%区间排除1。随词表增大差距收窄：128k时域内BPE配对比为0.983，域外1.025，FLORES为1.116。比率分解显示每字符token数比全程接近1，存活的只有字符级长度（SLP1散文1.028、韵文0.596）。部署实践下，梵语每命题消耗英语1.831–2.899个token。

Conclusion: 所谓梵语的分词效率优势主要是分词器与语料差异造成的假象；经匹配分词后真正保留的是字符级长度，而梵语散文在SLP1下相对英语并不更短。稳健的结论是：在当代散文与FLORES上，采用已部署的o200k英语枢轴并搭配SLP1梵语，梵语每命题消耗英语1.831–2.899个token，即高信息密度并不转化为分词层面的成本优势。代码、结果快照与全部表格均已公开。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [105] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 二元选择型真值基准存在表层特征泄漏，模型可借此超出随机水平而无需真正推理；本文揭示该问题并提出清理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择型真值基准要求模型在正确与错误答案间选择，但若两者在表层特征上系统性地存在差异，模型无需进行预期推理即可超过随机水平，从而高估模型能力。

Method: 在TruthfulQA上训练一个仅含六个特征的简单逻辑回归分类器，用于区分正确答案与错误答案；并检查其他基准中是否存在类似的表层伪影；进而提出一种通用清理机制Audit-Prune，通过移除最强化泄漏的答案对来消除伪影。

Result: 六特征逻辑回归分类器在TruthfulQA上能达到相当高的区分准确率；其他基准中也存在类似的表层伪影；经清理后，TruthfulQA的表层特征泄漏降至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 二元选择型真值基准的表层特征泄漏是可检测、可利用的，且可被下游分类器放大；Audit-Prune机制可在数据集发布前对其进行清理，为基准的可靠性提供保障。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [106] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TAM基准，评估大模型在长时程程序性推理上的能力，涉及ICD-10-CM编码与美国联邦量刑两个领域，结果显示现有方法准确率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多为短时程任务，只需少量检索或推理步骤，无法反映模型在真实场景中遵循数百页、规则相互依赖的手册时的可靠性，可能高估了大模型的推理能力。

Method: 构建TAM基准，从ICD-10-CM临床编码和U.S.联邦量刑两个领域精选真实任务，配有经人工验证的标签；每个任务需遵循含数万条规则的权威手册，跨章节执行一系列相互依赖的步骤以得出精确答案。评估了通用提示、检索增强生成、ReAct式提示及agent-harness基线（基于GPT-5）。

Result: 最佳精确匹配表现极低：ICD-10-CM编码仅1%，量刑任务仅15.5%。

Conclusion: 当前基准可能高估了LLM的推理能力，忽略了"可靠遵循长规则化流程"这一关键挑战；TAM数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [107] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kraken 语音到语音翻译模型，通过单层矢量量化低比特率 token 与 Autowave-X 解码器，缓解 LLM 高比特率语音 token 预测困难及训练数据约束，在翻译质量、说话人及韵律迁移上优于 SeamlessM4T-Large v2 与 Qwen2.5-Omni。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音 LLM 的语音到语音翻译（S2ST）虽有进展，但存在两大问题：一是在 LLM 中预测高比特率语音 token 较为困难；二是依赖说话人身份与韵律理想对齐的 S2ST 训练数据，数据约束较强。

Method: 采用基于单层矢量量化的低比特率 token，并以重建自监督学习（SSL）特征为目标进行训练；引入独立的 token 到波形解码器 Autowave-X，同时以源语音为条件以增强非语言信息迁移，从而放宽训练数据约束。整体模型 Kraken 在预训练 LLM 上增强语音特征输入与低比特率 token 输出，后接 Autowave-X 声码器，基于 Qwen3-8B 构建。

Result: 使用 15 万小时多语言、多任务语音数据进行训练，模型在翻译质量上优于 SeamlessM4T-Large v2 和 Qwen2.5-Omni，同时说话人与韵律迁移能力也有所提升。

Conclusion: 通过低比特率 token 与条件化解码器的结合，Kraken 有效提升了 S2ST 的翻译质量与非语言信息保留能力，为语音 LLM 的 S2ST 建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [108] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ESRL，一种面向 MoE 架构的强化学习框架，通过显式探索专家路由空间来提升 rollout 多样性，在多种路由方式和任务上取得最优性能且无额外采样或计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向 MoE 的 RL 研究主要关注优化稳定性和训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。然而路由决定了稀疏计算路径并影响输出分布，是 rollout 多样性的额外来源。作者通过实证分析发现，扰动专家路由能有效改变模型输出、增加 rollout 多样性（类似提高解码温度），但直接扰动会激活不合适的专家并显著降低 rollout 质量。

Method: ESRL 保留高置信度专家作为锚点，将随机路由限制在合理的候选池内，以保留可靠的计算路径；同时根据路由器熵自适应调整扰动强度以避免过度扰动。为缓解扰动带来的路由不匹配，ESRL 记录 rollout 期间使用的专家路径并在策略优化时重放。

Result: ESRL 在 top-K、top-1 和共享专家路由的多种 MoE 骨干上，以及数学、科学、代码任务中均取得最佳性能，且无额外采样或计算成本。在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上，ESRL 相较 GRPO 平均 Pass@1 和 Pass@8 分别提升 3.2 和 4.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过利用 MoE 特有的路由结构，ESRL 有效提升了 RL 训练效果。对专家利用率和训练动态的进一步分析揭示了这种路由结构利用方式的收益来源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [109] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Duplex Cue 评估框架，用于衡量全双工语音智能体在"轮内适应"（继续说话同时吸收听者贡献）的能力，而非仅判断其是否继续或停止说话。基于 300 条真实非脚本英语对话线索的案例研究显示，人类说话者在协作场景下的适应率达 68.2%，而 PersonaPlex 仅 34.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评估大多只关注智能体是"继续说话"还是"停止说话"这一二元判断，无法表达人类常用的第三种反应：在继续说话的同时融入听者刚刚提供的贡献（如缺失词、纠正或澄清）。这种轮内适应能力对自然语音交互至关重要，但缺乏相应评估手段。

Method: 提出 Duplex Cue 评估方法，将听者意图（反馈信号、协作、打断）与说话者行为（不变继续、轮内适应、让出话轮）分离，其中适应包括确认接收与内容修正。采用单模型案例研究，使用来自非脚本英语对话的 300 条人工确认线索，将人类响应的录音与在重放听者音频时生成的 PersonaPlex 续说进行对比，保留 208 对满足条件的样本（说话者在线索起始时仍活跃且两种条件下均有可评分响应）。

Result: 在 66 对协作线索中，录制的人类说话者有 68.2% 的情况发生适应，而 PersonaPlex 仅为 34.8%。该模型其余情况表现为不变继续（42.4%）或让出话轮（22.7%）。

Conclusion: 评估自然语音交互不仅需要测量智能体是否继续说话，还需要测量其如何回应听者的贡献。Duplex Cue 为这一类轮内适应行为提供了可操作的评估框架，并揭示了当前模型与人类表现之间在协作适应上的明显差距。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [110] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS)，一种门控稀疏注意力机制，通过将选择器的连续分数注入注意力logits，实现上下文排序与语言建模损失的端到端优化，在各任务和注意力预算下优于可训练稀疏注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可训练稀疏注意力方法使用轻量选择器评分并硬Top-K选择，切断了语言建模损失的梯度，只能蒸馏逐层稠密注意力分布。这种排序并未直接对齐固定注意力预算下各上下文单元对预测的实际影响，导致有限的预算被浪费在低价值单元上。

Method: 提出SAS门控稀疏注意力：训练时把选择器的连续分数以log形式注入注意力softmax内的门控中；使用归一化softmax门控来校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前块；保留连续选择器分数以学习相对优先级而非仅硬选择。为支持长序列训练，实现了一个集成SAS到FlashAttention风格计算的内存高效Triton内核。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上，SAS在多种注意力预算下持续优于可训练的稀疏注意力基线，在紧张预算下增益尤为显著，证明其对下游任务更有效的上下文排序能力。

Conclusion: 通过让选择器与语言建模损失端到端联合优化，SAS有效解决了稀疏注意力中排序与预测影响不匹配的问题，是一种简单而有效的后训练注意力稀疏化方案。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer在结构泛化上比词汇泛化更难并非其固有特性，而是由于既有数据集中词汇类型多样性高、结构类型多样性低所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作发现Transformer的结构泛化比词汇泛化更难，作者认为这一差异并非Transformer固有，而是源于数据集中的类型多样性偏差（词汇类型多、结构类型少），需要检验该假设。

Method: 将组合泛化分为词汇与结构泛化，定义类型多样性为某类型构造器数量；用Grammatical Framework为COGS和SLOG数据集构造语言学上多样化的变体，改变词汇与结构类型的多样性数量进行实验。

Result: 类型多样性与词汇、结构测试用例的组合泛化均同等相关，支持作者假设；并发现这与以往"复合散度解释泛化难度"的结论相矛盾。此外还考察了其他数据集属性（如非新测试结构的类型多样性、逻辑语义格式的表面属性）的影响。

Conclusion: 结构泛化更难并非Transformer固有缺陷，而是数据集类型多样性不均衡造成的；类型多样性对词汇与结构泛化的影响是等同的。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Test-Driven Approaches to Software Engineering with Large Language Models: A Survey of Phases, Tasks, and Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12012)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对87篇研究文献（83篇方法/协议级抽取）及5份实践资源进行结构化范围综述，围绕"测试改变了什么决策"这一问题，区分红-绿-重构循环与测试条件生成、执行引导精化、测试中介分析、仅评估测试等模式，并跨任务比较，提出机制分类学与协议敏感的证据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试日益参与大语言模型与软件工程智能体的决策，包括指定意图行为、指导程序构建与修复、筛选候选、约束变换及提供执行证据。这些用途虽源于测试驱动开发，但在测试顺序、预言可用性、可编辑产物及执行角色上差异显著，缺乏统一的组织框架与跨任务比较。

Method: 结构化范围综述：整合87条研究与支持记录，对其中83条进行方法级或协议级信息抽取，另收集5份实践资源。将红-绿-重构循环与测试条件生成、执行引导精化、测试中介分析、仅评估测试加以区分，并比较代码生成、修复、翻译、重构、克隆检测、代码搜索、定位、训练数据构建与形式化规约验证等任务；专项分析智能体工作流与可复用技能如何编码测试流程及其效果评估。

Result: 跨任务证据表明，测试可用性、测试有效性、反馈使用与评估独立性应被视为相互独立的属性；仅测试通过不能确立行为等价、有效反馈或流程遵循；聚合改进可能掩盖不同模型、任务与分母下的差异化结果。

Conclusion: 作者将上述区分综合为机制分类学、跨任务比较与协议敏感的证据分析，并指出未来研究方向：预言验证、因果评估、长时程维护与可复用测试驱动智能体能力。

Abstract: Tests increasingly participate in the decisions made by large language models and software engineering agents. They specify intended behavior, guide program construction and repair, select candidates, constrain transformations, and provide execution evidence for software analysis. These uses draw on test-driven development, yet differ substantially in test order, oracle availability, editable artifacts, and the role of execution. We present a structured scoping survey organized around the question of what decision a test changes. The review integrates 87 research and supporting records, with method- or protocol-level extraction for 83 records, alongside a separate collection of five practice resources. We distinguish the Red--Green--Refactor cycle from test-conditioned generation, execution-guided refinement, test-mediated analysis, and evaluation-only testing. We then compare code generation, repair, translation, refactoring, clone detection, code search, localization, training-data construction, and formal-specification validation. A dedicated analysis examines how agent workflows and reusable skills encode testing procedures and how their effects are evaluated. Across these tasks, the evidence supports treating test availability, test validity, feedback use, and evaluation independence as separate properties. Test passing alone does not establish behavioral equivalence, effective feedback, or process adherence; aggregate improvements can also conceal different outcomes across models, tasks, and denominators. We synthesize these distinctions into a mechanism taxonomy, a cross-task comparison, and a protocol-sensitive evidence analysis, and identify research directions in oracle validation, causal evaluation, long-horizon maintenance, and reusable test-driven agent capabilities

</details>


### [113] [When Agent Metrics Measure Different Things: An Evidence-Grounded Audit of the Praxa AI Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12017)
*Stefan G. Creadore,Peyton Woakz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 Praxa AI 的评测工件与运营记录进行回溯性测量审计，揭示评测数值正确但所测构念与标签不符的问题，并给出可复用的验证包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评测常出现"数值正确但测量构念错误"的情况，例如门禁策略豁免、生命周期计时被钳制、以及压缩收益只报后续调用而非整体调用，导致评测结论被过度解读为能力优势。

Method: 审计三类来源：139 例离线路由报告、包含 8,843 行工具尝试的无标识导出、以及单轨迹压缩试点记录。复现描述性计算，用独立的加权有理算术实现验证 91 项计时统计，并执行 13 项评分函数测试与 12 项分析验证器测试；用有限完成边界说明缺失时长的限制，不做数值插补。

Result: 路由报告 112 通过/27 失败但门禁零失败，因已知缺口被豁免；8,843 行中 8,395 有耗时、448 缺失，其中 121 条等于有符号 32 位最大值并带有"客户端放弃"标签，数据库代码对生命周期时长做钳制；合并记录的 99 分位为 2,147,483,647 ms，而服务器观测的已完成调用仅 38,118.31 ms，属分层对比而非处理效应；压缩试点报告后续输入减少 94.39%，但触发与后续调用合计仅减少 46.54%。

Conclusion: 贡献是带来源链接的案例研究与可复用验证包，用于把门禁策略、生命周期计时与请求级核算同更广泛的智能体性能主张区分开；历史供应商运行与完整当前流水线未独立复现，故一般性能力优越性与总体统计显著性均不成立。

Abstract: Agent evaluations can be numerically correct while measuring a different construct from the one implied by their labels. We present a retrospective measurement audit of selected Praxa AI implementation files, historical evaluation artifacts, and operational records. A 139-case offline routing report contains 112 passes and 27 failures despite zero gating failures, because known gaps are explicitly exempted from the gate. An identifier-free export represents 8,843 tool-attempt rows: 8,395 recorded durations and 448 missing values. Of the durations, 121 equal the signed 32-bit maximum and carry abandoned-client labels; inspected database code clamps elapsed lifecycle age. The pooled recorded 99th percentile is 2,147,483,647 ms, versus 38,118.31 ms among server-observed completed calls. This is a stratum contrast, not a treatment effect. In a documented single-trajectory compaction pilot, the reported follow-up input reduction is 94.39%, but the reduction across the trigger and follow-up calls together is 46.54%. We reproduce the descriptive calculations, verify 91 timing statistics through a separate weighted rational-arithmetic implementation, and execute 13 scoring-function and 12 analysis-verifier tests. Finite-completion bounds show how missing durations limit all-row timing statements without imputing values. The contribution is a source-linked case study and reusable verification package for separating gate policy, lifecycle timing, and request-level accounting from broader agent-performance claims. Historical provider runs and the full current pipeline were not independently reproduced; general capability superiority and population-level statistical significance are not established.

</details>


### [114] [Missing Dimensions: Integrating Human and Social Systems into Digital Twin Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12131)
*Francis Bordeleau,Mark van den Brand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文主张数字孪生工程应明确纳入工程、自然/生物、人类与社会四个系统维度，并认为随着孪生向人类与社会维度扩展，重心应从自动控制转向决策支持与使用者赋能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生已成为数字化转型的核心技术，但其工程实践视野过窄，仅聚焦于工程系统与自然系统，忽略了人类与社会维度所带来的独特属性、建模需求与伦理义务。

Method: 以观点性论述为主，提出四维系统框架（工程、自然/生物、人类、社会），逐一分析各维度的特性与建模要求，并以智能建筑作为贯穿性案例加以说明。

Result: 阐明四个维度在数字孪生中必须被显式识别；指出人类与社会维度的引入要求设计目标从自动化控制转向决策支持与居住者赋能；并提出多维数字孪生工程的四项开放研究挑战。

Conclusion: 数字孪生工程需拓展至四维系统视角，并以智能建筑为例验证其必要性；未来研究应围绕多维数字孪生的建模、伦理与赋能等开放挑战展开。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) have emerged as a key technology at the core of digital transformation, yet their engineering practice remains too narrowly focused on engineered and natural systems. This paper argues that four system dimensions must be explicitly recognized in DT engineering: Engineered, Natural/Biological, Human, and Social. Each dimension brings distinct properties, modeling requirements, and ethical obligations that fundamentally shape what a DT must represent and how it must be built. We further argue that as DTs extend into the Human and Social dimensions, the emphasis should shift from automated control toward decision support and occupant empowerment. We illustrate our arguments using a smart building as a running example and identify four open research challenges for multi-dimensional DT engineering.

</details>


### [115] [A decision-basis contract for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification: deterministic rules, verbatim evidence, and fail-closed abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12156)
*Jan Hölter,Kevin Geis,Benjamin Raab,Boris Bauke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"决策依据契约"的可审计LLM辅助医疗账单核验概念验证，将版本化费用目录的确定性规则检查与LLM自由文本语义评估分离，并在合成目录与36个案例上评估四个本地开源模型，发现端到端性能无一致优势，但显式文档要求与证据门槛提升了缺失信息识别与决策可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗账单核验需要既遵循版本化费用目录规则，又能判断自由文本文档是否支持申报项，同时要求决策过程可审计。纯端到端LLM方案缺乏可追溯的规则依据与证据链，难以满足审计与失败关闭需求。

Method: 提出决策依据契约，分两层：确定性层解析适用的目录版本并检查编码可用性、数量限制与排除项；语义层将每个申报项分类为支持、被反驳或缺少必要信息，支持与反驳均需逐字证据片段，规则上下文缺失、评估失败或证据缺失时通过失败关闭式弃权阻止支持。在合成目录与36个精选案例上比较四个本地开源模型，并设置无显式文档要求的消融和端到端基线。

Result: 结果一致性因模型而异，相较基线无一致优势；显式文档要求提升了全部四个模型对缺失信息的识别；证据门槛还暴露出原始判断正确但缺乏有效证据、从而被转为不完整决策依据条目的案例。

Conclusion: 显式决策记录可使规则发现、文档判断与弃权原因均可审查。实际价值仍需在真实目录、独立标注文档及人工评审条件下进一步评估。

Abstract: This work presents a proof of concept for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification based on a decision-basis contract. The contract separates deterministic checks of versioned fee-catalog rules from LLM-based assessment of free-text documentation. The deterministic layer resolves the applicable catalog release and checks code availability, quantity limits, and exclusions. The semantic layer classifies each claimed item as supported, contradicted, or missing required information. Support and contradiction require a verbatim evidence span; unavailable rule context, unsuccessful assessment, or missing required evidence prevents support through fail-closed abstention. We evaluated four locally run open-weight models on a synthetic catalog and 36 curated cases under the contract, an ablation without explicit documentation requirements, and an end-to-end baseline. Outcome agreement varied across models and showed no consistent advantage over the baseline. Explicit documentation requirements improved identification of missing information for all four models. The evidence gate also exposed cases in which correct raw judgments lacked valid evidence and were converted to incomplete decision-basis entries. The results show how explicit decision records can make rule findings, documentation judgments, and abstention reasons inspectable. Evaluation on real catalogs, independently annotated documentation, and with human reviewers is required to assess practical value.

</details>


### [116] [Open Source Stewardship Communities: "We need you, but not your pull request"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12236)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI降低了开源软件中实现变更的成本，但审查成本依旧高昂，导致部分项目开始限制谁可以贡献代码。作者提出"守护型社区"（stewardship community）概念：少数核心成员保留实现权，而更广泛的社区在不写代码的情况下塑造软件。论文追问当编码智能体取代外部贡献者的实现工作时，人类社区将如何自我更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于以人为本的AI软件工程视角下对开源软件可持续性的担忧。AI使实现变更的成本降低，但审查他人贡献的成本相对高昂，一些项目因此限制实现类贡献，转而欢迎其他形式的参与。作者关注的核心问题是：当编码智能体让维护者能够替代原本由外部贡献者提供的实现工作时，人类社区（尤其是开源社区的自我更新机制）会发生什么变化。

Method: 这是一篇观点/立场性论文（position paper），作者通过概念化的方式提出并命名"守护型社区"（stewardship community）这一新形态，即由一个小型核心保留实现权威，而更广泛的社区在不写代码的前提下继续塑造软件，且参与编码的准入逐渐依赖于审批而非自发贡献。作者以此现象为基础展开对以人为本软件工程含义的思辨性分析。

Result: 作者提出：在守护型社区中，参与编码的机会越来越依赖审批而非自发的贡献行为。这带来一个更广泛的问题——当编码智能体使维护者可以取代原本由外部贡献者提供的实现工作时，人类社区将走向何方。作者指出，仅让人类保持对AI智能体的控制是不够的，因为AI可能一边替代实现劳动，一边削弱开源社区自我更新的能力。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：对于以人为本的软件工程而言，仅仅让人保持对AI智能体的控制并不充分。AI在替代实现劳动的同时，可能削弱开源社区自我更新（renew themselves）的方式。因此，以人为本的软件工程需要关注的不只是人类对AI的掌控，还包括社区如何在新的人机分工下持续吸纳和培养新的参与者。

Abstract: Human-centric AI for software engineering means keeping humans responsible for work performed with AI. In Open Source Software (OSS), AI lowers the cost of implementing changes, but reviewing someone else's contribution remains comparatively expensive, so some projects now restrict who may contribute implementations while still welcoming other participation---not because the code is AI-generated, but because it no longer justifies the review cost. We call the resulting form a stewardship community: a small core retains implementation authority while a broader community continues to shape the software without writing code, and access to coding increasingly depends on approval rather than self-initiated contribution. This raises a broader question: what happens to the human community when coding agents let maintainers replace implementation work once supplied by external contributors? For human-centric software engineering, keeping humans in control of AI agents is not enough: AI can replace implementation labor while weakening how OSS communities renew themselves.

</details>


### [117] [PQLS: A High-Performance Python Library for Steady-State Simulation of Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12309)
*Evan Simanovskis,Raviraj Adve,Javane Rostampoor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PQLS是一个基于JAX/XLA的高性能Python库，用于并行求解Lindblad主方程的稳态解，支持大规模参数扫描并提供分层API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稳态求解器对每个参数配置顺序求解，Python层开销大、硬件利用率低，难以应对频率光谱、场依赖响应、原子速度分布多普勒平均等大规模或多维参数扫描需求。

Method: 提供三级分层API（高层接受原子阶梯系统物理描述并自动确定参数；中层接受Rabi频率、失谐、衰减率、网络拓扑等；低层直接指定哈密顿量与坍缩算符）。基于JAX和XLA，将多个哈密顿量表示为批处理张量，通过JAX编译求解器向量化、硬件加速处理。

Result: 在CPU和GPU平台上与QuTiP、QuTiP-JAX、RydIQule进行基准测试，在所有测试配置中均实现显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: PQLS通过分层API与批处理向量化求解，兼顾物理便利性与数值控制，有效加速Lindblad主方程稳态的大规模参数扫描计算。

Abstract: PQLS (Parallel Quantum Liouvillian Solver) is a high-performance Python library for computing steady-state solutions of the Lindblad master equation. It provides a layered user-facing API with three levels of abstraction. The high-level API accepts physical descriptions of atomic ladder systems and automatically determines the required model parameters; the mid- level API accepts quantities such as Rabi frequencies, detunings, decay rates, and network topology; finally the low-level API allows users to directly specify the Hamiltonian and collapse operators. This layered design enables users to choose between physical convenience and direct numerical control depending on the application. PQLS is built on JAX and XLA to enable vectorized and hardware-accelerated computation of large parameter sweeps. Rather than solving the steady-state problem sequentially for each parameter configuration, PQLS represents multiple Hamiltonians as batched tensors and processes them through a JAX-compiled solver, reducing Python-level overhead and improving hardware utilization. This is particularly useful for applications requiring large or multidimensional parameter sweeps, such as computing frequency spectra, evaluating field-dependent responses, and performing Doppler averaging over atomic velocity distributions. PQLS has been benchmarked against QuTiP, QuTiP-JAX, and RydIQule on CPU and GPU platforms, demonstrating substantial computational speedups across the tested configurations.

</details>


### [118] [A Retrieval-Augmented Automated Stakeholder for Requirements Elicitation Education: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12576)
*Manal Binkhonain,Ohoud Mosa Alharbi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将基于LangChain的检索增强生成（RAG）用于需求工程课程中的需求获取角色扮演，通过69名学生的对照实验表明，RAG模拟干系人虽不如真人真实有趣，但能促使学生提出更高质量的获取问题并识别更完整的功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈所需的软技能培养困难，角色扮演虽常用但受时间与资源限制；大语言模型虽可实现自动化交互式干系人模拟，却存在幻觉和响应不一致的问题，因此需要引入RAG来提升模拟的可靠性与一致性。

Method: 使用LangChain框架实现检索增强生成（RAG）的干系人模拟，在需求工程课程中开展对照实验，招募69名学生，分别与非技术背景的教师真人角色扮演者和基于RAG的模拟干系人进行需求获取交互，并对比两组表现。

Result: 学生认为受邀真人干系人更真实、更具参与感；但基于RAG的自动干系人使学生在需求获取中提出更高质量的问题，并识别出更完整的功能。

Conclusion: RAG可有效缓解LLM在需求工程教育中幻觉与响应不一致的问题，虽在真实感和参与度上不及真人，但在提升获取问题质量与功能识别完整性方面更具优势，可作为受资源限制时角色扮演的有效补充。

Abstract: Developing the skills required for requirements engineering students to conduct effective requirements elicitation interviews is critical yet challenging, as it requires the development of soft skills in addition to technical knowledge. Role-playing is widely adopted in requirements engineering education to support the development of these skills but is often constrained by time and resource limitations. Although recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable automated and interactive stakeholder simulations for role-playing, their application in requirements engineering education remains limited by hallucinations and inconsistent responses. To address these limitations, this study investigates the use of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), implemented using the LangChain framework, to support requirements elicitation activities in a requirements engineering course. We conducted controlled experiments with 69 students, comparing cohorts who interacted with non-technical faculty role-players and those who engaged with RAG-based simulated stakeholders. The results indicate that while students perceived invited stakeholders as more realistic and engaging, the RAG-based automated stakeholder produced higher-quality elicitation questions and more complete feature identification.

</details>


### [119] [What is the Difference Between Me and You? Benchmarking the Quality Gap Between Human-Written and AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12708)
*Cristina Improta,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文大规模对比了人类与AI生成代码的质量差异：AI代码结构更紧凑、风格更模板化，缺陷类型与安全表现因语言而异，并发布了CQBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手正成为生产软件的"共同作者"，但现有评估聚焦于功能正确性，忽略了决定软件生命周期成本的质量维度，因此需要探究AI代码与人类代码在这些维度上的差异。

Method: 构建787,562对函数样本，覆盖Python、Java、C三种语言：每个来自开源仓库的人类函数与其docstring由三种AI助手（OpenAI GPT系列、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen2.5-Coder）生成的实现配对。刻画结构复杂度与统计自然度，并将静态分析结果分别映射到正交缺陷分类（ODC）和通用弱点枚举（CWE），实现作者与语言间的直接可比。

Result: AI代码在结构上更压缩、风格上更模板化，规模与分支约为人写代码的一半，并在风格层面聚为一类。缺陷类型不同：人类代码集中于成熟代码库的问题，AI代码则多为重复性样板。安全性因语言而异：LLM在Python和Java中产生更多、更严重的告警，但在C语言中高严重度的内存安全发现少于人类。控制代码规模后，复杂度指标信号微弱，而自然度可有效区分作者。

Conclusion: AI与人类代码在质量维度上存在系统性差异，复杂度指标在控制规模后区分能力有限，自然度是更有效的区分信号；作者同时发布CQBench（含27,346个易出错任务、基线及评估流水线），以支持质量保障与安全测试。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are becoming co-authors of production software, yet their evaluation centers on functional correctness, leaving open whether their code differs from human code in the quality dimensions dominating lifecycle cost. We compare human-written and AI-generated code at scale: 787,562 function pairs across Python, Java, and C, each human function mined from open-source repositories paired with implementations generated from its docstring by three AI assistants (OpenAI GPT models, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen2.5-Coder). We characterize structural complexity and statistical naturalness, and map static-analysis findings onto Orthogonal Defect Classification for defects and the Common Weakness Enumeration for vulnerabilities, making authors and languages directly comparable. AI-generated code is structurally compressed and stylistically templated: roughly half the size and branching of human code, clustering apart at the style level. Defect profiles differ in kind: human code concentrates issues of mature codebases, AI code repetitive boilerplate; security is language-dependent, with LLMs producing more, and more severe, findings in Python and Java but fewer high-severity memory-safety findings than humans in C. Once size is controlled for, complexity metrics carry little signal, while naturalness separates authors. Finally, we release CQBench, a benchmark of 27,346 issue-prone tasks with baselines and an evaluation pipeline for quality assurance and security testing.

</details>


### [120] [GraphAHA: Graph-Based Adaptive Search with Heterogeneous Actions for Test-Time Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12757)
*Xitao Li,Haijun Wang,Gege Yuan,Qiyuan Wu,Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphAHA，一种带异构动作的自适应图搜索方法，用于提升代码生成的测试时扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法存在两个问题：树结构搜索将不同生成历史视为独立状态，即使轨迹收敛到同一程序也会重复评估、无法共享统计信息；同时采样、修复、推理三类动作收益互补且依赖状态，有限预算下难以在线分配。

Method: 将测试时代码生成组织为带类型的定向无环图，把等价程序合并为单个代码节点，使下游搜索统计可跨所有发现路径复用；再通过分层Thompson采样决定是生成新状态还是沿已有后继推进，并在生成时从类型合法的采样、推理、实现、修复操作中选择。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上，使用Qwen2.5-Coder与DeepSeek-Coder评估，GraphAHA在20个案例中的18个取得最佳分数；以可见测试测量的Pass@1在两个模型、两个基准上平均超过最强基线4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: GraphAHA通过等价程序合并与分层Thompson采样实现异构动作的自适应分配，能更有效地利用固定推理预算提升代码生成表现。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves code generation by spending additional inference budget (e.g., calls or tokens) on direct sampling, feedback-conditioned repair, and reasoning-guided implementation. Search-based methods can allocate this budget adaptively, but two challenges remain. First, tree-structured search treats each generation history as a separate state even when trajectories converge to the same program, duplicating evaluation and preventing statistics from being shared. Second, sampling, repair, and reasoning have complementary and state-dependent payoffs, making online allocation among them difficult under a finite budget. To address these challenges, we propose an adaptive graph search method with heterogeneous actions (GraphAHA). GraphAHA organizes the test-time code generation in a typed directed acyclic graph. Equivalent programs are merged into a single code node, allowing their downstream search statistics to be reused across all discovery paths. Hierarchical Thompson sampling then selects whether to generate a new state or follow an existing successor and, for generation, chooses among the type-valid sampling, reasoning, implementation, and repair operations. Evaluated on LiveCodeBench and CodeContests with Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, GraphAHA achieves the best score in 18 of 20 cases. For Pass@1 measured using visible tests, it outperforms the strongest baseline for both models on both benchmarks by 4.1 percentage points on average, demonstrating more effective use of a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [121] [Detecting HTTP Status Code Misuses in REST APIs via Static and Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12770)
*Alix Decrop,Andrea Arcuri,Mike Papadakis,Pierre-Yves Schobbens,Gilles Perrouin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出结合静态与动态分析的方法，检测REST API中HTTP状态码的误用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST基于HTTP，响应状态码虽已标准化，但其语义在REST中未被强制执行，导致实践中大量误用（如用500表示客户端错误）。这些误用会降低互操作性、误导API客户端，并使测试工具产生误报。

Method: 先研究2,625个真实REST API规范，识别相关状态码并基于HTTP标准与REST API原则推导出30条使用规则；再实现工具在OpenAPI规范（静态分析）和API行为（动态分析）中识别规则违规。

Result: 评估发现REST API中状态码误用频繁且具有系统性，静态与动态两种方法均能检测出多种误用。

Conclusion: 静态与动态方法可互补使用，研究也为REST API测试者和使用者提供了启示。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used on the web for client-server communications. As REST is based on HTTP, server responses contain status codes to indicate the outcome of requests (e.g., 200 OK for a success and 404 Not Found for an unavailable resource). While HTTP status codes are standardized, their semantics are not enforced in REST, leading to many misuses in practice (e.g., using 500 Internal Server Error to describe a client error). Such misuses may have nefarious consequences, such as reducing interoperability, misleading API clients, or causing false positives in testing tools. In this paper, we present a combined static and dynamic analysis approach for detecting HTTP status code misuses in REST APIs. We first study 2,625 real-world REST API specifications to identify relevant status codes and derive a set of 30 usage rules based on HTTP standards and REST API principles. We then implement tools to identify such rule violations in OpenAPI specifications (static analysis) and in API behavior (dynamic analysis). Our evaluation finds that status code misuses are frequent and systematic in REST APIs, with both static and dynamic approaches detecting various misuses. We highlight that both approaches may be used in a complementary manner, and also provide insight for REST API testers and users alike.

</details>


### [122] [Involving before Evolving: A Vision for Trustworthy Enterprise Digital Twin Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13071)
*Kérian Fiter,Adil Lagrou,Franck Dervault,Bentley Oakes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"先参与、后演化"的企业数字孪生（EDT）工程愿景，通过三阶段方法解决组织协同与信任问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数字孪生期望实现组织级的数据驱动决策支持，但落地时面临部门孤岛、隐性知识以及高影响长周期决策等挑战。现有方法虽在模型开发中引入领域专家，却忽视了对EDT的早期组织认同（buy-in）。

Method: 提出基于"involving before evolving"范式的可信EDT工程愿景，即先通过可运行原型快速让利益相关者参与，再向联邦与完全互操作演化。三阶段方法包括：利用基础模型进行快速原型构建、构建本体论骨干支持联邦互操作、以及可观测性工具以建立利益相关者信任。

Result: 该愿景依托与跨国制造企业Michelin的持续合作进行验证，初始原型已帮助获得利益相关者的认同。

Conclusion: 早期组织参与是可信EDT工程的关键前提，原型驱动的快速参与有助于为后续联邦互操作与信任构建奠定基础。

Abstract: Enterprise Digital Twins (EDTs) promise data-driven decision support at organizational scale, but realizing them requires navigating siloed departments, tacit knowledge, and high-stakes decisions with long-horizon consequences. Existing approaches involve domain experts during model development but focus less on early organizational buy-in in EDTs. We present a vision for trustworthy EDT engineering grounded in an `involving before evolving' paradigm: rapidly involving stakeholders through a working prototype before evolving toward federation and full interoperability. Our three-stage approach combines foundation models for rapid prototyping, an ontological backbone for federated interoperability, and observability tooling for stakeholder trust. We ground our vision in an ongoing collaboration with Michelin, a multinational manufacturer, where an initial prototype has helped support stakeholder buy-in.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Establishing the Four-Day Workweek: Understanding Adaptation and Long-Term Survival in an Agile Software Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13089)
*Michael Neumann,Darja Šmite*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对一家实行32小时四天工作制的敏捷软件组织进行2022与2026年两轮共15次访谈的纵向单案例研究，揭示四天工作制是不断演化的安排，其长期存续依赖员工的自主保护性适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有四天工作制研究多聚焦引入过程与短期效果，对其长期存续机制及其与敏捷软件开发的互动缺乏了解。本文旨在探究四天工作制在组织与外部环境变化下如何被引入、调整、制度化并维持。

Method: 采用纵向单案例研究，对象为一家实行32小时四天工作制的软件组织；数据来自2022年和2026年共15次半结构化访谈，使用定性内容分析。

Result: 四天工作制更像一种持续演化的安排而非一次性干预。引入后团队重新设计协调、沟通、会议、敏捷实践与迭代以适应工时缩短；制度化后遭遇所有权变更、经济下行、市场与AI压力，员工未回归五天制，而是通过自愿的保护性适应吸收压力，并预期回退会损害工作满意度、组织承诺与雇主形象。

Conclusion: 本文为敏捷软件开发中四天工作制的长期维持提供了纵向证据，并提出两个概念工具：四天工作制生命周期模型与四天工作制存续矩阵，用以解释外部压力与管理层逻辑如何影响回退风险。

Abstract: Context: Existing research on the four-day workweek (4DWW) has primarily examined its introduction and short-term effects, with limited understanding of its long-term survival or its interaction with agile software development. Objective: We study how a reduced-hour 4DWW is introduced, adapted, institutionalized, and sustained under changing organizational and external conditions in an agile software organization. Method: We conducted a longitudinal single-case study of a software organization operating a 32-hour, four-day week. The study draws on 15 semi-structured interviews in 2022 and 2026, analyzed using qualitative content analysis. Results: The 4DWW is better understood as an evolving arrangement than a one-off intervention. After the introduction, teams redesigned coordination, communication, meetings, agile practices, and iterations to adapt to reduced working time. Once institutionalized, the 4DWW faced ownership change, economic downturn, and market and AI pressures. Rather than reverting to five-day workweek, employees absorbed these pressures through voluntary protective adaptations, while anticipating that a rollback would harm job satisfaction, organizational commitment, and employer image. Contribution: We contribute longitudinal evidence on sustaining 4DWW in agile software development and propose two conceptual artifacts: a lifecycle model of the 4DWW and a 4DWW survival matrix, explaining how external pressures and management rationale rollback risk.

</details>


### [124] [Investigating Developer-Reported Software Security Testing Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12008)
*Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析 Stack Overflow 上 17,743 条软件安全测试相关问答，识别出 582 条相关问题并构建 8 大类、31 子类的分类体系，揭示了开发者面临的实际挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全测试对识别漏洞至关重要，但开发者在工具选择、环境配置、扫描结果解读、认证工作流测试及漏洞修复等方面面临诸多实际困难。研究旨在系统刻画开发者报告的 SST 挑战，弥补现有研究对真实实践问题认识的不足。

Method: 使用 SST 相关关键词从 Stack Overflow 收集 17,743 个问题，通过人工标注筛选出 582 条 SST 相关问题，构建 8 类 31 子类的分类体系；随后采用流行度、难度、相关性、时间趋势、共现和留出稳定性分析等方法进行实证刻画。

Result: 开发者频繁讨论安全发现解读与可靠性、安全测试指导与工具选择、认证授权测试、安全工具集成与自动化等话题。验证类问题通常需要更多技术背景和更长解决时间；安全发现解读与修复可操作性呈上升趋势。共现分析显示误报常与可解释性同时出现，集成问题常与工具建议和文档资源相伴。

Conclusion: 开发者报告的 SST 挑战超出漏洞检测本身，还涵盖工作流、配置、结果解读与修复等环节。这些发现可帮助研究者、实践者、教育者和工具提供方改进 SST 可用性、文档、结果解读与修复支持。

Abstract: Software security testing (SST) is essential for identifying vulnerabilities and improving software security, but developers often face practical challenges when selecting tools, configuring test environments, interpreting scanner outputs, testing authentication workflows, and acting on reported vulnerabilities. This study empirically characterizes developer-reported SST challenges in Stack Overflow (SO) discussions and examines their prevalence, difficulty, temporal evolution, co-occurrence, and taxonomy stability. We analyze 17,743 SO questions collected using SST-related keywords. Through manual labeling, we identify 582 SST-related questions and construct a taxonomy of 8 categories and 31 subcategories. We then analyze these challenges using prevalence, difficulty, correlation, temporal trend, co-occurrence, and held-out stability analyses. The results show that developers frequently discuss security finding interpretation and reliability, security testing guidance and tool selection, authentication and authorization testing, and security tool integration and automation. Validation-related challenges often require more technical context and longer resolution time. Security finding interpretation and remediation-related actionability show increasing trends over time. Co-occurrence analysis shows that false positives frequently appear with explainability, while integration challenges often appear with tool suggestions and documentation/resources. Overall, developer-reported SST challenges extend beyond vulnerability detection and include workflow, configuration, interpretation, and remediation concerns. These findings can help researchers, practitioners, educators, and tool providers improve SST usability, documentation, result interpretation, and remediation support.

</details>


### [125] [Is Bash All You Need? An Empirical Study of Tool Interfaces for Enterprise Digital Worker Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11999)
*Hazel Mak,Susheel Suresh,Sahil Bhatnagar,Barry Wang,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究比较了五种工具接口（typed tools、typed tools+bash、纯bash、带持久化合成工具的bash、PTC）在企业任务上的表现。结果显示纯bash在两个基准上均优于typed tools，且token消耗更少；PTC在安全合规受限场景下是备选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明基于shell的agent在编程任务上表现出色，但企业工作还涉及跨应用与服务切换、与同事协作及专业分析。作者希望检验通用shell在企业任务上是否能超越专用工具接口。

Method: 在TheAgentCompany和APEX-Agents两个基准上，使用Opus-4.8与GPT-5.5模型，对比五种工具接口：typed tools、typed tools+bash、纯bash、带持久化agent合成工具的bash，以及程序化工具调用（PTC，其运行程序的动作被限制在typed tool目录内）。

Result: 纯bash在两个基准上均优于typed tools：TheAgentCompany提升21.8-24.5个百分点，APEX-Agents提升4.8-7.4个百分点，同时总token消耗减少19-72%。在bash基础上增加typed tools或持久化工具合成未带来可检测的整体分数增益。PTC比直接typed调用消耗更少token且任务表现大致相当，但在质量与成本效率上通常不及纯bash。

Conclusion: 对企业实践者而言，当可对任意执行进行隔离时，建议采用纯bash；当安全或合规政策要求固定工具目录时，则应采用PTC。

Abstract: In this study, we examine whether a general shell can outperform specialized tools on enterprise tasks. Shell-based agents have shown strong results in coding, but enterprise work also involves moving between applications and services, coordinating with coworkers, and performing professional analysis. We compare five tool interfaces on TheAgentCompany and APEX-Agents using Opus-4.8 and GPT-5.5: typed tools, typed tools plus bash, bash alone, bash with persistent agent-synthesized tools, and programmatic tool calling (PTC), which runs programs whose actions are restricted to a typed tool catalog. Bash alone outperforms typed tools on both benchmarks, improving score by 21.8-24.5 pp on TheAgentCompany and 4.8-7.4 pp on APEX-Agents while using 19-72% fewer total tokens. Adding typed tools or persistent tool synthesis to bash produces no detectable pooled score gain. PTC uses fewer tokens than direct typed calls with broadly similar task performance, but generally underperforms bash alone in both quality and cost efficiency. For enterprise practitioners, these results favor bash alone when arbitrary execution can be isolated and PTC when security or compliance policies require a fixed tool catalog.

</details>


### [126] [Reality Is the Final Verifier: On Two Key Gaps in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12039)
*Alexander Krentsel,Shubham Agarwal,Mert Cemri,Shu Liu,Sidharth Sankhe,Ziming Mao,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"双缺口框架"（需求缺口与模型缺口），统一解释智能体软件工程中的主要失效模式（奖励黑客、幻觉），并主张目标应从"闭合缺口"转向"持续缩小缺口"，提出基于部署证据的"保障-修订循环"及资源分配视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发遵循"实现-验证"循环，但即便形式化证明也难保证部署后可接受的行为。原因在于需求仅近似利益相关者意图（需求缺口），模型仅近似真实部署环境（模型缺口），二者无法在开放变化的世界中被彻底闭合，因此需要新的理论框架来统一理解并应对这些失效模式。

Method: 提出"双缺口框架"统一需求缺口与模型缺口，用以解释奖励黑客（利用需求或模型遗漏）与幻觉（虚构需求或环境假设）等失效模式；进而提出"保障-修订循环"，利用部署证据在利益相关者拒绝行为时修订需求、模型或评估器；并将有保障的智能体开发建模为面向人类判断、智能体能力与算力的资源分配问题。

Result: 识别出两个主要瓶颈与两个缺口对应：需求缺口对应人类判断，模型缺口对应忠实且昂贵的评估。现实被视为最终验证者——实际部署条件下的可接受行为是终极检验，而部署前评估仅为其代理指标。

Conclusion: 由于缺口在开放动态世界中无法被证明闭合，目标应转为持续缩小缺口；应以部署证据驱动的保障-修订循环替代单纯的事前验证，并在人类判断、智能体能力与算力之间进行资源分配，最终以真实部署行为作为最终检验。

Abstract: Software development follows an implementation-verification loop in which developers or agents iteratively revise an implementation until an evaluator, such as a test suite, accepts it. The evaluator checks the implementation against a set of requirements under a model of the deployment environment. Yet even a formal proof that the implementation satisfies the requirements under the model cannot guarantee acceptable behavior after deployment. Requirements only approximate stakeholder intent, and the model only approximates the real deployment environment. We call these together - requirement gap and model gap - the two-gap framework, which unifies the main failure modes of agentic software engineer-ing: reward hacking exploits omissions in the requirements or model, while hallucination widens the gaps by fabricating requirements or environment assumptions.
  Because neither gap can generally be certified closed in an open, changing world, the goal shifts from closing them to continuously narrowing them. We therefore propose an assurance-revision loop that uses deployment evidence to revise the requirements, model, or evaluator when stakeholders reject the resulting behavior. We then cast assured agentic development as a resource-allocation problem over human judgment, agent capability, and compute. The two principal bottlenecks mirror the two gaps: human judgment for the requirement gap and faithful, costly evaluation for the model gap. Reality remains the final verifier: acceptable behavior under actual deployment conditions is the ultimate test, while predeployment evaluations remain proxies for it.

</details>


### [127] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Scientific Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12190)
*Aaron Nobile,Andreas Adelmann,Mohsen Sadr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探究能否用小规模开源模型构建完全本地的编程助手：通过RAG将昂贵计算前置到离线摄取阶段，在线回答阶段保持轻量。在IPPL C++科学代码库的100题基准上，9B模型取得最高均分0.795，超过更大模型及Claude Code检索架构下的同款模型。结论是模型家族与检索质量比参数量更重要，前置代码理解可让小型本地模型提供有据可依的仓库专属回答，适合作为隐私保护的内部科学代码开发工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前顶尖编程助手（如Claude Code、Codex）依赖超大规模云端模型，计算成本高且存在数据隐私风险。作者希望验证：能否通过把计算负担从推理阶段转移出去，用小型开源模型构建一个实用且完全本地的编程智能体。

Method: 构建面向科学代码理解的RAG系统，严格分离两个阶段：昂贵的离线摄取阶段（解析、结构图构建、LLM生成实体解释、嵌入）与轻量的在线回答阶段。在IPPL C++科学代码库上构建覆盖11个类别的100题基准，由独立的前沿模型担任评判对答案打分，共测试七种回答模型。

Result: 模型家族与检索质量比参数规模更重要：一个9B模型取得最高平均分0.795，既优于本流程中更大的模型，也优于嵌入Claude Code检索架构的同款模型。

Conclusion: 将代码理解前置并沉淀为可复用、面向特定代码库的向量存储，能让小型本地模型给出有依据、贴合仓库的回答，使该智能体适合作为内部科学代码库的隐私保护开发工具。

Abstract: Large language models have become central to modern coding assistants, but state-of-the-art systems such as Claude Code or Codex rely on very large, cloud-hosted models with significant computational cost and data-privacy implications. This work investigates whether a useful, fully local coding agent can be built around small open-source models by shifting the computational burden away from inference. We develop a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for scientific code understanding that strictly separates an expensive offline ingestion stage parsing, structural graph construction, LLM-generated entity explanations, and embedding from a lightweight online answering stage. The system is evaluated on a 100-question benchmark spanning eleven categories over the IPPL scientific codebase written in C++, with answers scored by an independent frontier model as the judge. Across seven answering models, we find that model family and retrieval quality matter more than parameter count, i.e. a 9B model achieves the highest average score (0.795), outperforming both larger models within our pipeline and the same models embedded in the Claude Code retrieval architecture. The results indicate that front-loading code understanding into a reusable, codebase-specialised vector store enables small local models to deliver grounded and repository-specific answers, making the agent well suited as a privacy-preserving development tool for in-house scientific codebases.

</details>


### [128] [Separating Engineering Reasoning from DEXPI Serialization in LLM-Based Greenfield Surface-Process Design: A Three-Case Study for Underground Gas Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12656)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这项诊断性研究比较了"直接生成DEXPI序列化"与"先生成轻量工程中间表示(IR)再序列化"两种方法，发现分离工程推理与标准序列化可大幅降低表示负担并隔离序列化失败，但不能消除工程不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能生成工程描述与结构化流程表示，但标准级序列化（如DEXPI XSD）会显著增加生成负担。作者希望探究：将工程推理与DEXPI序列化解耦后，表示失败与工程失败的分布是否会发生变化。

Method: 针对地下储气库的地面工艺设计（绿地约束场景），设置三个案例：单压注入、采出与外输、双压注入。对比两种条件：直接DEXPI生成 vs. 生成轻量工程中间表示(IR)。全部六个条件使用固定模型快照qwen3.8-max-0902，每条件完成一次托管生成；直接输出用XSD校验，IR输出用最小校验器检查。

Result: 直接DEXPI输出3个案例中2个XSD有效，IR输出3个全部结构有效。直接提示词输入约121.8k–121.9k tokens，而IR提示词仅617–699 tokens。工程可行性并非一致偏向IR：其中一个IR输出因显式冷却状态矛盾被否决。案例还揭示直接DEXPI的两种失败模式：工程不一致与标准级序列化失败。

Conclusion: 在所受评估的方法组合中，推迟DEXPI序列化可显著降低表示负担并有助于隔离序列化失败；但仅降低表示负担并不能消除工程不一致，工程推理质量仍需独立保障。

Abstract: Large language models can produce engineering descriptions and structured process representations, but standards-level serialization can substantially increase the generation burden. This diagnostic study examines whether separating engineering reasoning from Data Exchange in the Process Industry (DEXPI) serialization changes where representation and engineering failures occur in constrained greenfield surface-process design for underground gas storage. We compare Direct DEXPI generation with generation of a lightweight Engineering Intermediate Representation (IR) on three cases: single-pressure injection, withdrawal and export, and dual-pressure injection. All six conditions use one fixed model snapshot, qwen3.8-max-0902, with one completed hosted generation per condition. Direct outputs are XSD-valid in 2 of 3 cases, while all 3 Engineering IR outputs are structurally valid under a minimal validator. Direct prompt inputs contain approximately 121.8k-121.9k tokens, compared with 617-699 tokens for the Engineering IR prompts. Engineering feasibility does not uniformly favor the IR: one IR output is rejected for an explicit cooling-state contradiction. The cases also reveal two distinct Direct DEXPI failure modes: engineering inconsistency and standards-level serialization failure. The observed comparison shows that, in these evaluated method bundles, deferring DEXPI serialization substantially reduces representation burden and helps isolate serialization failure, but reducing representation burden alone does not eliminate engineering inconsistencies.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [A Survey on Quantum-Safe Cryptographic Mechanisms: Building Blocks and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11991)
*Ricardo Parizotto,Preeti Yadav,Marcus Freire,Anderson Tomkelski,Maycon Peixoto,Emmanuel Zambrini Cruzeiro,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了抗量子密码机制的研究现状、迁移应用与开放挑战，按安全机制和应用领域对现有方案进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机的出现可能破解广泛使用的公钥加密与数字签名算法，对现有基础设施构成威胁，因此亟需发展能够抵御量子计算安全风险的机制。但向新型安全机制迁移仍在进行中，面临识别受威胁应用、实现敏捷可扩展迁移流程等挑战。

Method: 作者对量子安全密码机制的最新进展进行综述，考察用于将量子安全机制集成到现有应用中的构建模块，并按其所采用的安全机制进行分类；随后系统梳理现有向量子安全方案的迁移工作，按应用领域（电信、物联网、区块链等）分类。

Result: 建立了两类分类体系：一是按安全机制对量子安全集成构建模块的分类；二是按应用领域对现有量子安全迁移方案的分类，涵盖电信、物联网与区块链等领域。

Conclusion: 总结了实现向抗量子机制成功迁移所需应对的挑战，以及推动该迁移的动机。

Abstract: The possible emergence of fault-tolerant quantum computers may enable widely used cryptographic algorithms to be broken. These algorithms for public key encryption and digital signatures could be exposed if an efficient algorithm is employed. This poses a threat to existing infrastructure, motivating the development of mechanisms that can withstand quantum-computer cybersecurity risks. However, the transition to new security mechanisms is still underway and faces many challenges, including identifying which applications are under threat and implementing agile, scalable migration processes. In this work, we survey the state of the art in quantum-safe cryptographic mechanisms, existing applications targeting migration, and open challenges. We examine the building blocks commonly used to integrate
  quantum-safe mechanisms into existing applications and categorize them by the security mechanisms they employ. Next, we systematically review existing migrations into quantum-safe schemes and categorize them by application domain, including Telecommunications, the Internet of Things, and Blockchains. Finally, we summarize the challenges that must be addressed to enable a successful migration to quantum-resistant mechanisms, as well as the motivations for pursuing this migration.

</details>


### [130] [A Dark Forest First Attack Is Rational Only If the Attacker Accepts It as Its Last](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12003)
*Harvey Dam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重构"黑暗森林"论证：将敌意对象设为"未被控制的预警或报复能力"，且攻击者的名单永不完备。据此，先发制人只有在其消除目标的时机收益不低于所有幸存者获知造成的暴露损失时才理性；而未被发现的隐藏者是无法被任何打击消除的"残余"，会使攻击者丧失全部赌注。故黑暗森林信徒一旦先发打击，就等于接受其为最后一次攻击。信标（METI）的作用是"见证"——发送者不必能打击任何人，只需能告知能打击的人，从而延迟或威慑敌对者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑暗森林模型把被探测的文明当作打击对象，并在攻击者已知的名单上推演打击；其主张克制的论证所依赖的步骤恰在其适用情形中失效。本文旨在修正这一对象与名单假设的缺陷，重新刻画先发打击的理性条件与信息传输（METI）的真实效应。

Method: 形式化建模：以"残余能力"（幸存者学习到打击事件并预警或报复的能力）为敌意对象，假设攻击者的名单永不完备；定义先发打击的理性条件为"所消除目标的时机收益≥每一幸存者获知带来的暴露损失"；将发送信号建模为向接收者提供关于发送者能力的后验信息，并证明发送者"能够预警"；据此推导信标对敌对接收者的延迟效应与伤害条件。

Result: ①能够导致攻击者毁灭的残余（直接打击或预警能打击者）会使攻击者失去全部赌注，而攻击者从未发现的行动者正是这样一种无法被任何打击消除的残余。②先发打击仅当等待会使移除目标的成本上升约等于攻击者全部赌注时才理性。③传输揭示发送者，给接收者的是关于其能力的后验而非一个目标，并证明发送者能预警。④信标会延迟每一个无法几乎确定能清除发送者力量的敌对者；免费信标仅在四种情形下损害发送者：被确定击杀、全赌注紧迫性、接收者不惧发送者能预警的任何人、存在发送者未察觉的开口。

Conclusion: METI应以"触及范围"和"接收者必须相信什么才会行动"来评判，而非以对惩罚的恐惧。信标通过"见证"实现威慑：发送者无需能打击任何人，只需能告知一个能打击的人。因此，先发打击对黑暗森林信徒而言是自我终结之举，而信标在多数情形下更多起延迟与威慑作用。

Abstract: The Dark Forest argument holds that a civilization that detects another should strike it at once, so detection, and any transmission that invites it, is danger. Existing models make the detected civilization the object of the strike and play the strike on a roster the attacker knows; where they advise restraint, the advice rests on steps that fail in the cases it is for. This paper takes the object of hostility to be remaining uncontrolled capacity to warn or retaliate, on a roster no attacker ever knows to be complete. A first strike is rational only if the timing benefit of what it removes is at least the disclosure loss from every leftover that learns of it. A leftover able to bring about the attacker's destruction, by striking or by warning one who can, costs the attacker its whole stake, and the actors the attacker has never found are such a leftover that no strike removes. A Dark Forest believer that strikes first therefore accepts the strike as the last attack it will ever make; the strike is rational only if waiting would raise the cost of removing the target by about the attacker's whole stake. A transmission that reveals the sender gives a receiver a posterior over the sender's capacity, not a target, and proves that the sender can warn. A receiver need not be hostile; for one that is, the sender is a witness it must pay for, so a beacon delays every hostile that cannot be nearly sure of removing the sender's forces, and a free beacon harms its sender only through sure kill, whole-stake urgency, a receiver that fears no one the sender could warn, or an opening hidden from the sender. Messaging to extraterrestrial intelligence (METI) is judged by reach and by what a receiver must believe to act, not by fear of punishment, and a beacon deters by witnessing: the sender need not be able to strike anyone, only to tell someone who can.

</details>


### [131] [Access Control as Verified Parse Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12488)
*Saranachon Iammongkol,Zhiyi Huang,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"类结果"（class result）：证明前向、无回溯的 EverParse 验证器可识别一个有界有限状态类，固定偏移字段与有界析取构成的访问控制决策函数属于该类，从而一次机器验证的证明可复用于该类中所有策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业安全网关在网络与策略决策之间的代码路径反复出现实现漏洞：手写执行逻辑偏离策略作者意图，边界处的临时请求解析器引入内存安全缺陷。这些 bug 都在部署的执行代码中而非策略中。现有方法要么不验证执行运行时，要么仅通过差分测试把形式模型与手写引擎相连。

Method: 将有界策略语言的决策函数编码进固定大小字节缓冲区，用 SMT 求解器一次性验证执行代码（覆盖所有字节值），证明验证器接受当且仅当决策函数接受（对任意策略、请求、会话成立）。在固定端点集上编辑规则内容无需重新证明；增加端点则重跑工具链；扩展语言需新证明。验证门平台无关，仅需 EverParse/Z3 与 C 编译器。在 seL4 微内核上部署，确保每个请求都经过该门且未验证组件无法破坏已验证的执行链。

Result: 实现了对该类策略的一次性验证，证明可复用到类内任意策略；在 seL4 微内核上完成部署演示，保证所有请求经过验证门且未验证组件无法篡改验证执行链。

Conclusion: 论文建立了对某一策略的忠实执行（faithful enforcement），而非证明策略本身安全。验证门平台无关，可复用性强，代表了从逐策略验证到类别级一次验证的转变。

Abstract: Commercial security gateways repeatedly ship implementation bugs in the code path between the network and the policy decision: hand-written enforcement logic that diverges from the policy author's intent, and ad-hoc request parsers at the network boundary that introduce memory-safety flaws of their own. In both cases the bug is in the deployed enforcement code, not in the policy. Existing approaches either leave the enforcement runtime unverified or connect a formal model to a hand-written engine only by differential testing.
  Our contribution is a class result: a forward-only, backtrack-free EverParse validator is a verified recognizer for a bounded, finite-state class, and access-control decision functions with fixed-offset fields and bounded disjunction belong to it, so one machine-checked proof transfers to every policy in the class rather than being re-established per policy. Concretely, we encode a bounded policy language's decision function into a fixed-size byte buffer and verify the enforcement code once---covering all byte values---with an SMT solver, proving the validator accepts if and only if the decision function accepts, for every policy, request, and session. Editing rule content over a fixed endpoint set then needs no new proof; adding endpoints reruns the toolchain; extending the language needs new proofs. We establish faithful enforcement of a policy, not that a policy is itself secure.
  The verified gate is platform-independent, requiring only EverParse/Z3 and a C compiler, whose correctness we assume. We demonstrate a deployment on the seL4 microkernel, which ensures every request passes through the gate and that unverified components cannot corrupt the verified enforcement chain.

</details>


### [132] [Omniscience for the Masses: New Threats in the Metaverse's Democratized World Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12554)
*Andrea Mengascini,Ryan Aurelio,Jason Polakis,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估元宇宙世界创作者的安全与隐私风险，通过对25个支持用户创建世界的平台进行分析，设计并实现了五种新型攻击，证明创作者可仅凭标准创作功能突破空间、视觉和听觉约束，实现隐蔽监视与操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙平台依赖用户生成的虚拟世界实现快速增长与创意，但这一模式将社交互动和世界行为的控制权交给了不受信任的用户。目前缺乏对世界创作者所带来的安全与隐私风险的系统性研究。

Method: 调研25个支持用户创建世界的平台，分析其世界创建能力；据此设计并实现五种新型攻击，利用创作者提供的工具突破沉浸式环境中的空间、视觉和听觉约束；并验证五种已有攻击能否仅用标准创作功能复现；最后评估现有平台的审查、运行时保护和创作者政策。

Result: 五种新型攻击均可在无软件漏洞、无开发者权限的情况下实现隐蔽的用户监视与操控；五种先前提出的攻击也能仅凭标准世界创作功能复现；现有平台审查机制、运行时保护和创作者政策均不足以阻止恶意创作者行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户隐私期望与世界创作者被赋予的权力之间的根本性错配，表明元宇宙世界创作者构成一个此前被忽视的重要威胁面，亟需重新审视平台的权限授予与防护机制。

Abstract: Metaverse platforms increasingly derive their success from user-generated virtual worlds: self-contained social and interactive environments, which can be created by any ordinary user and scale to billions of visits. Platforms such as Roblox, Horizon Worlds, and VRChat now host millions of creator-built worlds that govern how users see, hear, and interact with one another. While this model enables rapid growth and creativity, it fundamentally delegates control over social interactions and world behavior to untrusted users. In this paper, we present the first systematic security and privacy assessment of metaverse world creators. We survey 25 platforms that support user-created worlds and analyze their world-creation capabilities. Guided by this analysis, we design and implement five novel attacks that exploit creator-provided tools to violate spatial, visual, and auditory constraints in immersive environments, enabling covert user surveillance and manipulation without software vulnerabilities or developer-level privileges. We further show that five previously-proposed attacks can be replicated using only standard world-creation features. Finally, we find that existing platform vetting, runtime protections, and creator policies are insufficient to mitigate malicious world-creator behavior, revealing a fundamental mismatch between users' privacy expectations and the powers granted to world creators.

</details>


### [133] [MicroHasTEE: Bare-Metal Haskell for Type-Level Peripheral Ownership on Armv8-M](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12580)
*Robert Krook*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MicroHasTEE 是一个多方编程框架，用单一类型化 Haskell 程序表达 TrustZone-M 安全与非安全固件，通过类型级能力账本与索引化设置计算在编译期排除跨域不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Armv8-M TrustZone 虽隔离安全与非安全软件，但开发者仍需跨两份独立构建的固件协调外设归属、中断路由、初始化和网关接口；两镜像间的假设不一致可能编译通过却仅在设备上以故障形式暴露。

Method: 将两个固件应用表达为同一 Haskell 程序中的参与方；用类型级能力账本表示外设权限，用索引化设置计算跟踪资源获取、配置、转移与终结；用领域特定效应类型限制外设操作与中断回调只能由持有相应权限的参与方执行；用类型化可调用句柄描述非安全代码可用的安全服务；由 MicroHs 将共享程序编译两次，生成独立的裸机安全与非安全固件镜像。

Result: 在 STM32U5 Nucleo 板上实现，含 TrustZone 配置、外设驱动与跨域 Haskell 调用的序列化网关；对通过其接口表达的程序，可拒绝不一致的资源使用、配置后改变归属、错误域中的回调以及调用未注册安全服务。门锁案例验证可行性，两镜像分别占用 232.7 KiB 与 228.4 KiB flash，每域约 220 KiB SRAM。

Conclusion: MicroHasTEE 以类型化多参与方编程将 TrustZone-M 跨镜像一致性问题前移到编译期，并在真实硬件上以可接受资源开销验证了实用性。

Abstract: Arm TrustZone for Armv8-M isolates Secure and Non-secure software, but developers must still coordinate peripheral attribution, interrupt routing, initialization, and gateway interfaces across separately built firmware images. Inconsistent assumptions between these images can compile successfully and emerge only as faults on the target device.
  We present MicroHasTEE, a multiparty programming framework that expresses both firmware applications as participants in one typed Haskell program. MicroHasTEE represents peripheral authority with type-level capability ledgers and uses indexed setup computations to track resource acquisition, configuration, transfer, and finalization. Domain-specific effect types restrict peripheral operations and interrupt callbacks to the participant that holds the corresponding authority, while typed callable handles describe the Secure services available to Non-secure code. MicroHs compiles the shared program twice to produce separate bare-metal Secure and Non-secure firmware images.
  We implement MicroHasTEE for an STM32U5 Nucleo board, including TrustZone configuration, peripheral drivers, and a serialized gateway for cross-domain Haskell calls. For programs expressed through its interface, MicroHasTEE rejects inconsistent resource use, attribution changes after configuration, callbacks in the wrong domain, and calls to unregistered Secure services. A door-lock case study demonstrates feasibility, with firmware images occupying 232.7 KiB and 228.4 KiB of flash and approximately 220 KiB of SRAM per domain.

</details>


### [134] [A Feature-Rich Embedded NIDS with eBPF/XDP: Detector and Architecture Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12605)
*Shiqi Wu,Oleksii Koshovyi,Georgios Pseiridis Pseiras,Victor Morel,Romaric Duvignau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并在Ericsson合作下开发了一个面向传输网络的DDoS检测NIDS：以统计基线为基础，用Isolation Forest结合GoFlowMeter提取的流量特征提升检测效果，并用eBPF/XDP在内核层过滤真实流量；随后在树莓派5上对比单体、Kafka与gRPC三种部署。结果显示检测质量主要取决于检测器选择，Isolation Forest显著优于基线，而gRPC架构在保持几乎同等精度的同时仅增加不足2毫秒开销，Kafka则精度落后约9个百分点、开销约27毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击对传输网络构成严重威胁，近期攻击量已超过30 Tbps，电信行业是主要目标。现有研究尚未探讨托管软件架构对网络监控方案的影响，也未评估近期改进攻击检测的算法，因此需要研究检测质量与架构开销之间的权衡。

Method: 在与Ericsson合作下开发传输网络NIDS：以统计基线为起点，使用在更广泛流特征上训练的Isolation Forest提升检测效果，特征由开源Go实现的CICFlowMeter（GoFlowMeter）提取；集成eBPF/XDP在内核层过滤真实流量。在Raspberry Pi 5测试床上回放CIC-DDoS2019数据集作为真实网络流量，比较单体、基于Kafka和基于gRPC微服务三种部署方式。

Result: 检测质量主要由检测器选择而非传输方式决定：Isolation Forest相比基线提升了召回率和F1分数（单体变体实时达到0.965），能够检出基线漏掉的低流量攻击窗口。传输方式并非中性：gRPC几乎达到与单体相同的准确率，每窗口仅增加不到2毫秒传输时间；异步Kafka流水线则落后约9个百分点，并增加约27毫秒开销。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了在资源受限硬件上部署NIDS时检测质量与架构开销之间的权衡，表明应优先选择更强的检测器（如Isolation Forest），而在需要微服务架构时gRPC优于Kafka。

Abstract: Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks remain a serious threat to transport networks, with recent attack volumes exceeding 30 Tbps, and the telecommunications industry being the main target. Recent work has yet to study the impact of the hosting software architecture on network monitoring solutions, or to assess recent algorithms for improving attack detection. This paper presents a Network Intrusion Detection System (NIDS) for DDoS detection in transport networks, developed in collaboration with Ericsson. Building on a statistical baseline, we improve detection effectiveness with an Isolation Forest trained on a wider set of flow features, extracted by GoFlowMeter, our open-source Go implementation of CICFlowMeter, and we integrate eBPF/XDP so that the NIDS filters real traffic at the kernel level. We further compare three deployments, monolithic, Kafka-based, and gRPC-based microservices, on a Raspberry Pi 5 testbed replaying the CIC-DDoS2019 dataset as real network traffic. Detection quality is governed mainly by the choice of detector rather than by the transport: the Isolation Forest raises recall and F1 score (0.965 live in the monolithic variant) over the baseline by flagging low-volume attack windows that the baseline misses. The transport is not neutral, however: gRPC reaches almost the same accuracy as the monolithic variant while adding less than 2 milliseconds of transport time per window, whereas the asynchronous Kafka pipeline trails by roughly nine percentage points and adds about 27 milliseconds. These findings clarify the trade-off between detection quality and architectural overhead when deploying a NIDS on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [135] [Poster: Towards Selecting Threat Appropriate Industrial Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12646)
*Stefan Lenz,Johannes Weidmann,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于反威胁情报共享的机制，用于根据当前威胁态势选择合适入侵检测器，以应对工业控制系统中动态变化的安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统面临的威胁态势是动态变化的，因此有效安全防护需要检测策略能够及时应对不断演变的威胁情况。现有入侵检测系统在不同攻击场景下表现不一，单一检测器难以适应所有情况。

Method: 提出一种基于反威胁情报共享（counter-threat intelligence sharing）的机制，用于为当前环境选择适当的检测器；并对多种入侵检测系统进行攻击层面的性能评估。

Result: 评估结果显示，入侵检测性能会随攻击场景的不同而变化，说明不同检测器在不同攻击下表现存在差异。

Conclusion: 入侵检测性能随攻击场景变化的事实强调了此类机制对工业控制系统安全的价值，基于反威胁情报共享的检测器选择机制具有应用潜力。

Abstract: As the threat landscape against industrial control systems is dynamic, effective security requires detection strategies capable of timely reactions to these evolving threat situations. To address this problem, we propose the idea of a counter-threat intelligence sharing based mechanism to select appropriate detectors for current circumstances. To highlight the potential of this mechanism, we conduct attack-level performance evaluations of various intrusion detection systems. Results show the variance of intrusion detection performance depending on the attack scenario, emphasizing the benefits of such a mechanism for industrial control system security

</details>


### [136] [Bridging the First-Hour Gap: Evaluating AI Reliability and Benchmarking Deficiencies in Cyber Incident Response for Law Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12681)
*Roshin Sleeba C,Hiran V Nath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了辅助网络犯罪一线响应的决策支持架构（剧本、LLM、RAG、Agentic AI），指出RAG是较可行的中间方案，但存在提示敏感性和法律场景幻觉风险，并论证现有基准不足，需建立聚焦"第一小时"证据保全的新评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络事件发生后的"第一小时"中，一线执法人员的操作至关重要，微小失误可能造成不可逆影响，导致调查完整性受损、网络犯罪起诉受阻。这主要源于数字证据的易失性，易引发程序错误与证据流失。同时现实中执法人员技术能力有限、取证基础设施参差不齐，因此需要评估何种决策支持架构能真正适用。

Method: 对面向网络犯罪第一响应者的决策支持架构进行系统性综述，将其分类为四类：剧本（playbooks）、大语言模型（LLMs）、检索增强生成（RAG）框架、以及Agentic AI系统；并结合实际场景中技术熟练度受限和取证基础设施不一致的约束进行批判性分析。此外，还审查了网络安全领域的现有评测基准。

Result: 分析识别出RAG系统因其自然语言适应性而成为相对可行的中间方案。但存在显著风险：提示（prompt）敏感性，以及法律语境下"自信的幻觉"（confident hallucinations）带来的重大挑战。同时发现现有网络安全基准不足以覆盖执法所需的特定安全与法律要求。

Conclusion: 作者主张有必要建立一个新的评测基准，聚焦于朴素查询鲁棒性（naive query robustness）和证据保全（evidence preservation），以确保AI驱动的指导与司法程序的强制性要求相一致。

Abstract: The actions of frontline law enforcement officers in the initial hour of a cyber incident play a vital role in determining the ultimate success of an investigation. The minor mistakes they commit might result in irreversible critical impacts. The integrity of the investigation can be compromised, and the prosecution of cyber criminals can be hindered due to minor mistakes that happen in the initial hour. These are mainly because of the volatile nature of digital artifacts that might lead to procedural errors and evidence attrition. This paper provides a systematic survey of decision-support architectures designed to assist first responders of a cybercrime, categorizing them into playbooks, Large Language Models (LLMs), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) frameworks, and Agentic AI systems. The survey critically considers the constraints of limited technical proficiency and inconsistent forensic infrastructure in a practical scenario. Our analysis identifies RAG-based systems as a relatively viable intermediate solution due to their natural language adaptability. However, significant risk factors like prompt sensitivity and the potential for confident hallucinations in legal contexts pose a major challenge. Furthermore, we review current benchmarks in cybersecurity and demonstrate that they are not sufficient to capture the specific safety and legal requirements of law enforcement, focusing on the initial hour of the cybercrime. We conclude by arguing for the necessity of a new evaluation benchmark focused on naive query robustness and evidence preservation, so as to ensure that AI-driven guidance aligns with the mandatory demands of judicial proceedings.

</details>


### [137] [Fresh-Challenge VDF Attestations for Model-Relative Response Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12727)
*Ansar Yesmukhanov,Aruzhan Tlessova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Fresh-Challenge VDF Attestations (FCLA) 协议组合，通过将 VDF 与不可预测的公共挑战、消息及可独立审计的发布/接收记录绑定，使有限验证者能够在明确假设下获得关于顺序计算响应延迟的公开证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独的 VDF 证明可被预先计算，无法证明延迟是在某挑战之后才产生；因此需要一个协议让有限验证者获得关于顺序计算响应延迟的公开、模型相对的证据。
