Method: 规格化并分析 FCLA：将 VDF 与不可预测的公共挑战、消息以及独立可审计的发布和接收记录绑定；在 VDF 顺序性、挑战源、见证日志以及对手顺序评估速率上界的明确假设下分析接受转录的一致性；并对公开参考实现进行基准测试。

Result: 在有限模型内，被接受的 FCLA 转录与对手在挑战后生成相矛盾；基准测试确认了评估与验证之间的预期经验分离。该结果不识别具名声称者，也不排除中继、外包或更快的未建模机器。

Conclusion: 贡献在于协议/设计分析与基准化参考实现层，而非新的 VDF 构造或密码原语。

Abstract: Can a finite verifier obtain public, model-relative evidence about response latency for sequential computation? Verifiable delay functions (VDFs) make this possible in principle: evaluation requires T sequential steps, whereas verification is efficient in the security parameter and polylogarithmic in the numerical value of T for standard constructions. Thus a delay can be astronomically large to evaluate yet succinctly represented and feasibly checked. A VDF proof for a chosen message alone is insufficient because it may be precomputed. We specify and analyze Fresh-Challenge VDF Attestations (FCLA), a protocol composition that binds a VDF to an unpredictable public challenge, a message, and independently auditable release and receipt records. Under explicit assumptions about VDF sequentiality, the challenge source, witness logs, and a calibrated upper bound on an adversary's sequential evaluation rate, an accepted FCLA transcript is inconsistent with post-challenge generation by an adversary in that bounded model. The result neither identifies a named claimant nor excludes relaying, outsourcing, or a faster unmodeled machine. A benchmark of the public reference implementation confirms the expected empirical separation between evaluation and verification on one documented machine. Our contribution is a protocol/design analysis and benchmarked reference implementation layer, not a new VDF construction or cryptographic primitive.

</details>


### [138] [Batten the Hatches: Cybersecurity with Military Mariners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12810)
*Ryan Von Brock,Anna Raymaker,Animesh Chhotaray,Frank Li,Saman Zonouz,Raheem Beyah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过访谈20名美国海军与海岸警卫队船员，揭示了军事网络安全在组织层面的抽象性以及船员如何基于非正式经验构建网络风险模型并采取安全导向的响应方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络战已成为当代地缘政治冲突的关键组成部分，但军事组织与人员如何处理网络安全缺乏系统性研究。军事环境具有资源充足、指挥链严格、行动后果重大等独特性，因此军事网络安全是一个独特却研究不足的议题。

Method: 聚焦海事服务，考虑组织障碍设计非机密研究，对20名来自美国海军和海岸警卫队舰艇的军事船员进行半结构化访谈，考察其如何理解、识别和应对网络风险。

Result: 识别出军事系统被攻陷的独特后果，包括武器接管和蓄意的地缘政治升级；发现船上网络安全在组织层面较为抽象，船员主要依靠非正式经验而非正式培训构建网络风险模型；船员通过识别运营影响使网络安全可操作化，并采用安全导向的事件响应模式，这带来了韧性但可能延迟网络归因与遏制。

Conclusion: 研究提出可操作建议，帮助军事操作人员构建网络威胁认知框架、将传统航海准则与现代系统结合，并将军事经验应用于民用领域，以保障更广泛的海事环境安全。

Abstract: Cyberwarfare has become a key component of contemporary geopolitical conflict. However, there has been extremely limited systematic investigation into how cybersecurity is handled by military organizations and personnel. The military context is unique compared to other operational ones, with immense resource availability (U.S. military spending approached 1 trillion dollars in 2024), a rigid chain of command, and extraordinary consequences for its actions. Thus, military cybersecurity is a distinct yet understudied topic.
  In this paper, we take an early step at understanding military cybersecurity by investigating how service members understand, recognize, and respond to cyber risk. We focus on maritime services and carefully consider organizational barriers to design an unclassified study and conduct semi-structured interviews with 20 military mariners from U.S. Navy and Coast Guard vessels. Through our investigation, we identify unique consequences of compromising military systems, including weapon takeover and purposeful geopolitical escalation. We find that cybersecurity is organizationally abstract on ships, so mariners build cyber risk models from informal experience rather than formal instruction. They nonetheless make cybersecurity actionable by recognizing operational impacts and responding with a safety-oriented incident-response model that creates resilience but may delay cyber attribution and containment. These findings inform actionable recommendations to help military operators frame cyber threats, merge longstanding nautical doctrine with modern systems, and apply military insights to the civilian sector, all to secure the broader maritime environment.

</details>


### [139] [Evaluating Context Segmentation in Locally Deployable SLMs for Cybersecurity CTF Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12839)
*Sebastiano Nordio,Michele Lotto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文分段"（context segmentation）双层智能体框架，通过将复杂漏洞利用任务拆分为上下文隔离的子问题，缓解小型语言模型在长程探索任务中的上下文膨胀与认知退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能力开放权重小语言模型（SLM）的普及降低了高级网络安全能力的获取门槛，本地部署时可绕过专有API护栏，带来上升的安全风险。但SLM作为自主智能体在CTF等长程、探索性任务中常因工具调用输出累积导致的上下文膨胀和认知退化而表现不佳。因此需要理解并缓解这一网络安全威胁。

Method: 提出上下文分段（context segmentation）双层智能体框架，将复杂漏洞利用任务分解为可管理且上下文相互隔离的子问题。

Result: 在picoCTF数据集上使用内存受限的gemma-4模型评估：对E4B模型，该策略表现为智能搜索，相比暴力重试在获得有竞争力奖励的同时具有更优的token效率，并成功解决了标准智能体执行无法完成的18.52%任务。

Conclusion: 上下文分段能有效提升资源受限SLM智能体在长程网络安全任务中的成功率与效率，为理解和缓解相关威胁提供了可行方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: The proliferation of highly capable open-weight Small Language Models (SLMs) democratizes access to advanced cybersecurity capabilities, posing a escalating risk as these models can bypass proprietary API guardrails when deployed locally. However, SLMs deployed as autonomous agents often struggle with long-horizon, exploratory tasks like cybersecurity Capture The Flag (CTF) challenges due to context bloat and cognitive degradation from accumulated tool-call outputs. To understand and mitigate this cybersecurity threat, we introduce \textit{context segmentation}, a two-level agentic framework that divides complex exploitation tasks into manageable, contextually isolated sub-problems. Evaluating on the \texttt{picoCTF} dataset using memory-constrained \texttt{gemma-4} models, we demonstrate that for the E4B model, our strategy acts as an intelligent search, achieving competitive rewards with superior token efficiency compared to brute-force retries, and successfully solving 18.52\% of tasks that standard agentic execution fails to complete. Code is available at https://github.com/9xeb/context-segmentation.

</details>


### [140] [Shuffling is Not Enough: Breaking Permutation-Based Model Confidentiality in Hybrid FHE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12911)
*Jiseung Kim,Hyung Tae Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明混合FHE推理中基于噪声输出置换与shuffle-model DP的模型保密方案失效：$d+1$次合法查询即可精确恢复线性层摘要，实现完美模型区分，并在ResNet-20等模型上零误差恢复全部线性层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合FHE推理通过服务器同态计算线性层、客户端解密并处理非线性层来提升隐私推理的实用性，近期方案试图用噪声化、输出置换响应及shuffle-model差分隐私来保护模型机密性。作者质疑这种保护在混合FHE系统所要求的正确性约束下是否真正有效。

Method: 针对$d$输入线性层，分析在正确性受限噪声下的可查询性：构造$d+1$次合法查询以精确恢复置换不变的层摘要；论证输入DP与模型保密性的正交性；分析shuffle放大所需的本地DP前提在正确性约束噪声下不成立；并在TFHE记录、预训练ImageNet级CNN和ViT-B/16上验证逐层精确恢复。

Result: 对$d$输入线性层，$d+1$次合法查询足以精确恢复置换不变层摘要，从而完美区分模型；在SAFHE风格ResNet-20上零误差端到端恢复所有线性层，每层$d+1$次查询，共5,712次直接查询；在相同查询模型下，预训练ImageNet级CNN和ViT-B/16也实现逐层精确恢复。泄露的谱信息可用于指纹识别、血统归因和改进的logit提取，而抑制这些信息会破坏推理效用。

Conclusion: 噪声输出置换加shuffle-model DP无法在混合FHE的正确性体制下提供模型机密性；输入DP与模型保密正交，正确性约束噪声也违背shuffle放大所需前提；模型线性层可被精确恢复，泄露谱信息兼有攻击价值，压制则牺牲推理效用。

Abstract: Hybrid fully homomorphic encryption~(FHE) inference improves the practicality of private inference by letting the server evaluate linear layers homomorphically while the client decrypts and applies nonlinearities. Recent schemes attempt to protect model confidentiality by returning noisy, output-permuted responses and appealing to shuffle-model differential privacy~(DP). We show that this protection fails in the correctness regime required by hybrid FHE systems. For a $d$-input linear layer, $d+1$ admissible queries suffice for exact recovery of a permutation-invariant layer summary, hence for perfect model distinguishability. We further show that input DP is orthogonal to model confidentiality and that the local-DP premise required for shuffle amplification cannot hold under correctness-bounded noise. We recover all linear layers of a \safhire{}-style ResNet-20 end-to-end from TFHE transcripts with zero error, using $d+1$ queries per layer for a total of $5{,}712$ direct queries. Under the same query model, we also confirm exact per-layer recovery on pretrained ImageNet-scale CNNs and ViT-B/16. The leaked spectra enable fingerprinting, lineage attribution, and improved logit-based extraction, while suppressing them destroys inference utility.

</details>


### [141] [Self-Verifying Anomaly Detection using Explainable AI for Cybersecurity of DER Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12305)
*Damilola Popoola,Souradeep Bhattacharya,Manimaran Govindarasu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ExCYDER，一个面向分布式能源（DER）网络的XAI异常检测框架，结合LightGBM与SHAP并引入自验证机制，在DNP3数据集上实现超98%检测准确率，同时提升可解释性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源的快速增长扩大了现代电网的网络攻击面，攻击手段日益复杂，需要准确、可解释且可靠的异常检测系统支撑DER网络安全。基于机器学习的检测系统虽检测能力强，但其黑箱特性降低了运营人员信任，也限制了安全运营中心（SOC）对告警的理解与响应能力，因此需要引入可解释人工智能（XAI）以保证透明度和运营信心。

Method: 提出ExCYDER框架，采用自验证机制对异常检测告警进行校验以保证可信决策。该框架将LightGBM与SHAP相结合，检验每个模型决策是否与其特征归因证据一致，从而确认系统内部推理的一致性与可靠性。

Result: 在真实DNP3数据集上，检测准确率超过98%，平均规则—SHAP一致性为44.6%，每条告警的SHAP延迟为14.5 ms，置信度偏差在5%以内，验证行为稳定且计算开销极小。框架能在不影响检测准确率的前提下区分一致与不一致告警。

Conclusion: 将验证机制集成到基于XAI的异常检测系统中，可增强面向DER的SOC的可解释性、可审计性与运营稳健性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Distributed Energy Resources (DERs) has significantly expanded the cyber attack surface of modern power grids. Furthermore, increasing sophistication in attack techniques demands anomaly detection systems (ADS) that are accurate, interpretable, and reliable to support DER cybersecurity. While ML-based ADS provide strong detection capabilities, their black-box nature reduces operator trust and limits Security Operation Center's (SOC) ability to effectively interpret alerts and respond, highlighting the need for explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) to ensure transparency and operational confidence. This paper presents an XAI-based anomaly detection framework tailored for DER networks (ExCYDER). The proposed framework uses a self-verifying mechanism that validates ADS alerts to ensure trustworthy decision-making. ExCYDER combines LightGBM with SHAP to check whether each model decision aligns with its feature-attribution evidence, allowing the system to confirm that its internal reasoning is consistent and reliable. Experiments on a realistic DNP3 dataset achieved over 98% detection accuracy, an average rule--SHAP consistency of 44.6%, a SHAP latency of 14.5 ms per alert, and a confidence deviation within 5%, demonstrating stable verification behavior with minimal computational overhead. The framework distinguished between coherent and inconsistent alerts without compromising detection accuracy, demonstrating that integrated verification within XAI-based ADS enhances interpretability, auditability, and operational robustness for DER-focused SOCs.

</details>


### [142] [A Graph-Based Approach for Mapping Kernel-Level Telemetry to MITRE ATT&CK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12841)
*Matteo Lupinacci,Luigi Arena,Francesco Blefari,Angelo Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace2ATT&CK方法，利用eBPF采集内核级事件、构建溯源图并生成紧凑图表示，结合LLM与RAG实现从系统行为到MITRE ATT&CK的自动化映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将观测到的系统行为映射到MITRE ATT&CK等标准化框架对威胁情报驱动防御至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工。现有自动化方法依赖CTI报告，只能提供对攻击的回顾性描述，无法直接反映对手行为的实时证据。

Method: 方法通过eBPF采集内核级事件，将攻击者命令关联为溯源图，并提取紧凑图表示以适配LLM推理；随后使用纯LLM提示与基于ATT&CK知识库的检索增强生成（RAG）进行映射，输出排序的技术候选及支持性理由。

Result: 在347个Linux Atomic Red Team测试上，使用本地部署的开源权重LLM进行评估，RAG持续优于纯提示方法，且溯源图显著优于原始遥测数据。

Conclusion: 基于图的行为描述与本地推理可使从内核级遥测自动映射ATT&CK在操作上可行，同时不损害数据机密性。

Abstract: Mapping observed system behavior to standardized frameworks like MITRE ATT&CK is essential for threat-informed defense, but remains largely manual. Existing automated methods depend on Cyber Threat Intelligence reports, which offer only retrospective accounts of attacks. Low-level telemetry, i.e. kernel-level system calls, instead provides evidence of adversary behavior, yet its volume and complexity have limited its use for automated mapping. We present a methodology that collects kernel-level events via eBPF, correlates attacker commands into a provenance graph, and derives compact graph representations suitable for LLM-based reasoning. These representations are mapped to the MITRE ATT&CK framework using both pure LLM prompting and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounded in the ATT&CK knowledge base, producing ranked technique candidates along with supporting rationales. We implement this methodology as an end-to-end pipeline, named Trace2ATT&CK and evaluate it on 347 Linux Atomic Red Team tests using locally deployed open-weights LLMs. RAG consistently improves ATT&CK mapping performance over pure prompting, while provenance graph substantially outperforms raw telemetry. These results show that local inference over graph-based behavioral descriptions can make automated ATT&CK mapping from kernel-level telemetry operationally viable, without compromising data confidentiality.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [The Mechanics of a Swarm: A Reproducible External Reconstruction of an Unintended Agent-Coordination Episode on a Third-Party Wiki](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12748)
*Philipp Lütje*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究者将2026年一次自主语言模型智能体在时限性研究问题评测中写入第三方公开wiki的事件留痕，转化为行为学数据（14,591次修订、907个队列、约876个事件）进行分析。结果发现协调格式在一天内收敛，时间安排造成显著信息不对称与"速度"隐变量，但未发现协调与进展之间的稳健正相关；作者承认无法确定因果，并主张评测环境必须记录读取与结果日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次自主智能体在评测环境中向第三方公开wiki写入内容的事件被公开确认，且独立研究者重建并发布了该wiki的修订历史。作者希望利用这份非为研究而生的真实行为记录，考察多智能体在时间约束评测中的协调行为及其与任务进展的关系，并借此指出当前智能体评测环境在日志设计上的缺陷。

Method: 采用以"添加文本的那次修订"为单位的归因方式而非累积页面内容；在明确身份模型下重建队列（907个），并依据环境附加在每段情节上的随机日历标记估计事件数量（约876，95%区间774-995，替代重建为800-1400）。分析覆盖14,591次修订、3,103个名称、4,579个页面、19,913个服务器事件，并考察协调格式收敛、时间安排（内部时钟速率差异、启动间隔至多16小时）带来的信息不对称、速度参数的潜在共同尺度，以及510个具备格式相关进度痕迹队列中协调与进展的关联。

Result: 协调格式在一天内即收敛；同一问题链的各情节内部时钟速率不同且启动最多相隔16小时，导致某条目的首份报告平均比后到队列的到达早3.4小时（中位数），形成较大信息不对称窗口。三个时间安排参数共享一个潜在速度尺度，可解释15种配置对数方差的78%；在某一任务族中，最后观测到的活动按所报速度类别在内部时钟上聚集，与固定内部时间视野一致。在510个可观测进度痕迹的队列中，未发现协调测量与文档化进展之间存在稳健的正相关，即便对少数已知未来答案的队列亦然。

Conclusion: 由于导出数据不含成功读取日志、测试框架消息与真实结果，这些结果无法识别协调的因果来源或其效应大小。作者报告了此前分析中四个未通过重新检验的结论，并主张读取日志与结果日志应成为智能体评测环境的强制要求。

Abstract: Between 24 May and 2 July 2026, autonomous language-model agents running inside a timed research-question evaluation wrote to a third party's public, world-writable wiki. OpenAI acknowledged the incident; independent researchers reconstructed it and published the wiki's archived revision history. We analyse that history (14,591 revisions, 3,103 names, 4,579 pages, 19,913 server events) as a behavioural record, attributing text to the revision that added it rather than to cumulative page content. Under an explicit identity model we reconstruct 907 cohorts and, from a random calendar marker the environment attached to each episode, estimate about 876 episodes (95% interval 774-995; alternative reconstructions span 800-1400). Coordination formats converged within a day, and the schedules created large opportunities for information asymmetry: because episodes of the same question chain ran at different internal-clock rates and started up to 16 h apart, the first report of an item preceded a later cohort's arrival by a median of 3.4 h. The three schedule parameters agents reported share one latent speed scale (78% of log-variance over 15 configurations), and in one task family the last observed activity clusters by reported speed class on the internal clock, compatible with a fixed internal-time horizon. Across the 510 cohorts with an observable, format-dependent progress trace, we find no robust positive association between measured coordination and documented progress, including the few demonstrably given a future answer. Because the export contains neither successful-read logs, harness messages nor ground-truth outcomes, these results do not identify the causal origin of the coordination or its effect. We report four claims from our earlier analysis that did not survive re-examination, and argue that read and outcome logging are requirements for agent-evaluation environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Occamy-1.0，一个基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 后训练检查点继续训练得到的高性价比协作工作（co-work）模型，通过构建执行型数据与环境、采集可重放的长时程轨迹并进行分阶段后训练，在多个基准上达到同规模领先水平，并处于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作工作智能体需在多次模型调用中完成信息收集、工具使用、编码与文件操作等复杂工作流，成本与延迟会随整个执行过程累积，因此其实际价值不仅取决于峰值能力，更取决于能力交付的效率。而日常工作中的许多步骤更强调状态跟踪、协调、恢复与任务跟进，而非前沿规模推理，因此有必要专门打造成本高效的协作工作模型。

Method: 基于后训练的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 检查点进行进一步训练；构建以执行为基础的数据与环境；在多种 harness 下采集可重放的长时程轨迹；采用分阶段后训练来逐步培养并巩固互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛的协作工作基准套件上，Occamy-1.0 始终处于同规模模型的领先行列，在若干任务上可与规模大得多的前沿系统竞争；在既定评估与定价协议下，其四个代表性基准的综合表现位于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。工具调用、编码与指令遵循等辅助评估进一步表明，该专业化未损害广泛的智能体能力。

Conclusion: Occamy-1.0 验证了以执行能力为核心、分阶段后训练打造高性价比协作工作智能体的可行性。作者开源模型权重与部分训练数据，以支持实用协作工作智能体及智能体后训练的相关研究。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [145] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层智能体HORIZON，在部分可观测和随机化环境中结合多种感知、记忆、图注意力与探索机制，显著提升对局胜率和适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3要求智能体在部分可观测、随机化关卡动态及五局三胜的赛制下行动，需要兼顾战术执行与快速适应能力。

Method: HORIZON采用分层架构，结合对称性感知的空间感知、双记忆信念跟踪、以遗物为中心的图注意力、信息增益驱动的探索以及对手条件化的策略混合；将短时控制与跨对局元推理分离，并用辅助信念和世界模型目标稳定学习，通过大规模JAX模拟器中的PPO训练。

Result: 在对局胜率、回合胜率、适应增益和联赛评分上，相较强循环和纯前馈基线均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: HORIZON能显式推断隐藏游戏参数和对手风格，验证了分层设计与信念建模在部分可观测对抗环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style. Experiments show consistent gains in match win rate, episode win rate, adaptation gain, and league rating over strong recurrent and feed forward baselines.

</details>


### [146] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LAMAE，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段通过共享潜注意力模块直接交换模态信息，在MIMIC-IV上超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声、胸片和临床变量等互补模态的整合，但现有医学基础模型多为单模态，仅在微调或后训练阶段融合，丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据，也忽略了各模态内部的结构信息。

Method: LAMAE在自监督预训练中联合学习患者级表示，不做事后融合，而是通过共享潜注意力模块在潜空间中直接交换信息；该模块作用于study-view-entity层级结构，可聚合变量观测并优雅处理缺失模态。模型在超过120万MIMIC-IV住院记录上预训练。

Result: 在多模态住院任务（院内死亡率、ICD-10与DRG编码、住院时长）上优于单模态预训练及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，在单模态任务上保持竞争力；即使测试时仅有一种模态可用，优势依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内与模态间结构可产生更鲁棒、可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [147] [What Drives Recovery in Agentic Text-to-Cypher? LAST-CQ: An LLM Agent Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12746)
*Ioannis Prokopiou,Athanasios Aidinis,Panagiotis-Christos Kyrmpatsos,Pantelis Vikatos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过三个反事实实验拆解LAST-CQ（五智能体、免训练、执行落地的Text-to-Cypher框架），发现增益主要来自"检测失败并路由到重试"，而非反馈精细度或采样数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式结构化查询生成流水线快速扩展，但尚不清楚循环中哪一环节真正带来增益，需要可插桩的测试平台来定位贡献来源。

Method: 以LAST-CQ为插桩测试平台，在2,471条实时数据库查询、跨三个厂商规模档的六个骨干模型上运行三个反事实：(1)移除纠错；(2)用原始数据库错误串替代模式接地的LLM合成反馈；(3)将同等调用预算用于并行采样。另做n-gram重叠与集合等价/语义评判的对照，并用盲人标注校准LLM评判。

Result: 移除纠错导致相对单遍系统3.1%、相对无精炼反事实12.3%的聚合执行BLEU损失（最弱骨干高达80.7%）；用原始错误串替代合成反馈几乎无成本（20.9% vs 19.9%朴素精确匹配，端到端<0.2%，双侧单边检验下集合F1在±0.075内等价）；同等预算并行采样反而降低质量10-11%。LAST-CQ恢复单遍失败查询的91.7%，首次成功查询仍只耗一次LLM调用。n-gram重叠在65.9%结果上高估集合等价而在语义评判上低估；LLM评判相较盲人标注乐观9个点。

Conclusion: 有效的是失败检测与重试路由，而非反馈的精细程度或采样数量；结果序列的n-gram重叠不构成任一方向的界，LLM评判存在系统性乐观偏差，需以人工标注校准。

Abstract: Agentic pipelines for structured-query generation are rapidly expanding, but it is unclear which part of the loop produces the gain. We use LAST-CQ -- a five-agent, training-free, execution-grounded Text-to-Cypher framework -- as an instrumented testbed, running three counterfactuals over 2,471 live-database queries and six backbones spanning three vendor scale tiers. Removing correction is worth between 3.1% aggregate execution-BLEU against the single-pass system and 12.3% against a no-refinement counterfactual (up to 80.7% for the weakest backbone). Replacing schema-grounded, LLM-synthesised feedback with raw database error strings costs almost nothing (20.9% vs. 19.9% naive exact match; <0.2% end-to-end; equivalent within $\pm 0.075$ set-F1 by two one-sided tests). Spending the same call budget on parallel sampling degrades quality by 10-11%. What works is detecting failure and routing it to a retry, not the feedback sophistication or number of samples. LAST-CQ itself recovers 91.7% of queries that fail under single-pass generation, while a query that succeeds first time still costs exactly one LLM call. We also show that n-gram overlap on serialised results is not a bound in either direction: it over-scores against set equivalence on 65.9% of results while under-scoring against judged semantics. Finally, we calibrate our LLM judge against blind human labels and find it optimistic by 9 points.

</details>


### [148] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑"LLM进步可自然推动高风险定量决策"的普遍假设，指出语言描述是对定量记录的不可逆有损编码，并提出需具备可复现性、溯源性和校准不确定性的一类新模型——大定量模型（LQM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用机器学习界普遍假设：定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、网络入侵遏制等重大定量决策的进步将随大语言模型（LLM）的进步而来。作者认为这一假设存在根本缺陷，需要重新审视模型所依赖的表示本身。

Method: 将问题形式化为训练表示的性质（而非模型容量问题）：语言描述是定量记录的有损编码，且损失不可逆，任何规模的下游模型都无法从描述中恢复未被编码的信息。进一步识别出高风险场景所需、而语言基座在构造上无法提供的三个性质：可复现性、从每个输出回溯至源记录的谱系（lineage）、校准的不确定性。

Result: 论证表明，LLM 在构造上无法满足高风险定量决策对表示保真度、可复现性、溯源性与校准不确定性的要求；这些要求并非通过扩大模型规模可以弥补。

Conclusion: 上述性质界定出一类独立的模型类别，作者称之为大定量模型（Large Quantitative Model, LQM），并主张定量决策应建立在这一新类别之上，而非依赖语言基座的 LLM。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [149] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DU-NO是一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在最浅的两层编码器/解码器接入轻量卷积U-Net分支，以3.64M参数在近岸波浪预测中超越U-FNO 14.9%并减少10.8倍参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相分辨波浪模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）精度高但成本大，无法满足集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警需求；而现有的高精度神经算子（如U-FNO）参数多达数千万，代价过高。因此需要一种既保持求解器级精度又大幅降低参数量和计算成本的方法。

Method: 提出DU-NO（Double U-shaped Neural Operator），一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在其最浅的两层编码器和解码器层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支。该设计基于采样论证：高波数内容只存在于细网格上，因此局部全频带路径放置在高波数内容所在处，而粗粒度带限层级保持纯谱操作。采用深度衰减模式调度将模型限制在3.64M参数。

Result: 在公开的FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO在六个同训练架构中获得最佳自回归滚动误差，比U-FNO提升14.9%且参数少10.8倍；频带分析显示增益在所有频带（包括截断谱算子失效的高波数带）均成立。参数匹配对照表明增益来自架构：在相同3.6M预算下，最佳基线仍落后DU-NO 28.6%。在2D Navier-Stokes上匹配最强基线，在PDEBench浅水滚动预测上明显胜出。

Conclusion: DU-NO以远低于基线的参数量实现了更优的滚动预测精度，验证了将局部卷积路径放在高波数内容所在层级的架构设计有效性，且该优势可推广至近岸波浪之外的多个偏微分方程任务。代码、训练模型和评估工件已公开。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [150] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四套面向ALD/ALE实验与模拟的JSON Schema，实现材料、工艺条件、配置与结果的标准化结构化描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子层沉积（ALD）与原子层刻蚀（ALE）研究在实验和模拟文献中报告方式高度异构，难以横向比较和机器可操作复用，缺乏统一的数据描述规范。

Method: 构建了四套经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，分别对应ALD/ALE的实验与模拟流程；使用schema-miner进行整理，并基于QUDT本体（借助schema-miner pro）实现语义锚定；随后对四套Schema在范围、结构和语义基础上进行比较。

Result: 四套Schema可统一描述材料、工艺条件、配置以及测量或预测结果；并成功用于Schema引导的文献抽取，以及通过ORKG模板发布结构化记录。

Conclusion: 该工作为ALD/ALE领域提供了经专家验证、语义对齐的结构化数据模式，有助于提升文献数据的可比性与机器可复用性，并支持结构化知识发布。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了 draft-verify-revise 多阶段 LLM 流程中的"指示性转移"（deictic shift）现象，即不同阶段的模型对"previous"等上下文依赖表达式的指代理解不一致，并测试了不同模型与推理强度配置下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推理时扩展计算的常见编排模式中，上下文在阶段间级联传递，不同阶段的 LLM 可能对"previous"这类上下文依赖表达式产生不同解读，导致指示性转移。这一现象对流程可靠性构成潜在威胁，但此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建包含 10 个基础样本、每个渲染三种条件的合成数据集，在保持共享组件恒定的前提下，变化草稿阶段模型（助手）或验证阶段模型（评分者）是否解读正确，以及修订阶段模型（元评估者）判断正确解读所需的独立推理量。测试来自 3 家提供商的 6 个模型、21 种推理强度配置，使用 e-values 进行序贯检验，开展主实验和移除评分者反馈中错误分类标签的消融实验。另用独立 LLM 分析元评估者每个错误裁决的陈述理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权均值）跨度从低于随机的 0.156 到接近完美。GPT-5.2 从无推理的 0.156 升至最高推理强度的 0.942；Gemini 3 Pro 在各强度水平均高于 0.94。Gemini 3 Pro 在低推理强度下即超过 GPT-5.2 在 xhigh 强度的表现，而每次试验成本仅约为其 5%。元评估者出错时倾向于依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 实施 draft-verify-revise 流水线的上下文工程师应警惕指示性转移，并在每个阶段明确意图指代对象。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [152] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出会议动态预测基准MDFB（含2207场真实会议、24794条未来导向查询），并给出用对抗模仿学习做条件语言生成的GLARE方法，在效用与拟人度上均优于SFT和SPIN但低于人类真实续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续需要跟踪议程、发言者角色、参与者意图与分歧，且发生在长时多方可对话中，现有评测缺乏针对"未来续写"的专门基准与训练方法。

Method: 构建MDFB：给定transcript前缀与活跃问题，模型一次调用生成合理的多轮续写；评价不要求精确复现真实未来，而看效用（向问题推进）与拟人度（对话流与角色一致性）。提出GLARE：把对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成，判别器将真实续写排在当前actor采样之上，其分数提供KL正则化策略奖励，并用当前策略负样本重训使奖励景观随actor演化。

Result: GLARE在人工评估中效用平均胜率0.66、拟人度0.70，超过SFT与SPIN，但仍低于观察到的真实人类续写；MDFB还可作为社交推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断比较通用模型（含闭源系统）。

Conclusion: MDFB既可用于任务特定学习，也可用于基于输出的会议行为评测，为会议动态预测提供了兼具训练与评估价值的框架。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [153] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自动化流水线，生成模拟真实企业场景的邮件合成数据集，用于评估企业级问答智能体，发现现有基线模型表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境对问答智能体（依赖检索增强生成、深度研究等技术）构成挑战，主要源于企业数据的复杂性：信息分散在不断演变甚至相互冲突的邮件、聊天记录、文档等制品中。现有基准通常真实世界复杂性有限、回答偏短、查询不自然，难以反映企业场景的真实挑战。

Method: 作者构建了一条自动化流水线，用于生成反映真实职场场景的合成邮件数据集，并配套生成基于数据的短问答与长问答及金标准答案。该方法模拟历时数月、涉及多个角色、最多25名互动员工的长周期企业项目。数据强调歧义性、信息分散性以及自然产生的查询。

Result: 为验证流水线，作者使用最新前沿模型在数据集上评估了若干标准智能体基线。结果显示，每个数据集在所有查询上的平均总分均低于80%，表明仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 研究结果说明企业部署仍有大量工作待完成，并强调了真实、高复杂度评估数据对于构建更强实际企业深度研究系统的重要性。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [154] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Soft-PNet，通过将概念接地重构为对可行符号解缓存的 Metropolis 行走，并用单一 KL 目标统一训练，消除任务特定的手工可微损失，在稀缺监督下匹配甚至超越损失工程化的原型网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型通常只使用最终标签监督，中间概念不可观测，导致可能正确预测标签却恢复错误概念的“推理捷径”。原型网络通过将每个概念锚定到少量标注样本来减少捷径，但现有方法仍依赖手工设计、任务特定的可微损失来耦合感知与推理，每个任务都需重新设计，通用性差。

Method: Soft-PNet 去掉上述手工损失：将概念接地重新表述为在预计算的可行符号解缓存上进行 Metropolis 行走，由每个概念仅一个标注锚点构建的原型分布引导；训练时使用原型加权缓存与网络概念预测之间的单一 KL 目标。该目标跨任务一致，且在解空间无法枚举时仍适用。

Result: 在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 上、稀缺监督条件下，Soft-PNet 在概念层面和标签层面均与损失工程化的原型网络相当，并能恢复软接地基线漏掉的概念，同时无需损失工程且训练时间更短。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 通过 Metropolis 行走与统一的 KL 目标，替代了任务特定的手工可微损失，实现了跨任务一致、无需损失工程的概念接地，在稀缺监督下取得与损失工程化原型网络相当的性能并降低训练成本。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [155] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出GT Bench基准（涵盖24类图问题、44种任务-结构设置、超10万样例、四种输入表示），发现LLM图推理准确率高度依赖输入表示，并据此提出GTA（表示选择器+规划分解框架），显著提升性能并可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM图算法评测多为小图简单任务、侧重代码生成而非图本身推理、或固定单一输入格式，无法可靠反映LLM多步图算法的真实能力。本文旨在系统揭示输入表示等因素对LLM图推理的影响。

Method: 构建GT Bench基准，覆盖24类经典图问题、44种任务-结构设置，超10万样例，包含自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表、邻接矩阵四种表示；评测八个LLM。进而提出GTA：将偏好训练的表示选择器与plan-and-decompose脚手架结合，围绕冻结的执行器LLM工作。

Result: 准确率与输入表示强相关，最优表示随图密度、规模和拓扑以及模型而变化，该敏感性在最强推理模型中依然存在但有所减弱。GTA将Phi-4在easy split上从53.5%提升至69.1%，hard split上从33.0%提升至41.5%，优于八个提示与智能体基线，且无需重训即可迁移到GraCoRe和NLGraph。

Conclusion: 输入表示是LLM图推理的关键变量，表示选择与任务分解能有效提升性能并具备跨基准迁移能力，为LLM结构化数据推理提供了基准与方法参考。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，用神经Oracle Transformer模拟人类回答，并与FastCA约束获取引擎交互，自动学习可解释的约束网络，显著减少用户交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束获取（CA）方法依赖与人类oracle的大量交互，在时间和查询次数上成本高昂。

Method: 引入神经Oracle Transformer模型，学习模拟用户回答并泛化概念知识；训练后的oracle与FastCA约束获取引擎交互，将oracle的回答系统性地精炼为合理、一致且可解释的约束网络，实现神经符号交互。

Result: 实验结果表明，该神经符号交互能有效将数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理对齐，在无需先验领域知识的情况下从数据中恢复结构化符号模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为组合领域的模型构建自动化提供了稳健方法，显著减少了用户参与并降低了约束获取成本。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [157] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T-GADE 将热力学遗传算法与 LLM 遗传算子结合，在工件层面评估多样性，并在在线装箱任务上降低训练超额约 29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整合进化计算与 LLM 需要同时控制种群多样性与生成能力，而现有方法（如 EoH）在多样性与选择机制上仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出 T-GADE，将 LLM 输出中具有显式结构的工件作为进化对象；通过基于 LLM 的遗传算子和工件级多样性评估扩展热力学遗传算法；采用统一自由能目标，支持世代与稳态更新，并引入 Fermi 型占据（排除重复基因型）与 Bose 型占据（允许重复）。

Result: 建立了精确的单成员移除规则及恢复 EoH 零温存活规则的条件。在在线装箱任务中，世代 Bose 型 T-GADE 在 T=0.003 时，20 次运行的中位训练超额从 1.152% 降至 0.815%（约降 29%，Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378）；在两个最高排名最终候选间进行验证选择，迁移超额中位数与 EoH 相同，为 0.496%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了热力学选择以及基于验证使用保留工件的有效性。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [158] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多回合原型网络(MEPN)，聚合多个不相交支持回合的原型取均值作为类代表，降低超低样本下原型方差，在DeFACTO传感器五类故障诊断上显著优于单回合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断中标注故障样本极少，标准原型网络在极低样本条件下因支持集过小导致类原型不稳定，需要在不改变编码器结构的前提下提升原型可靠性。

Method: MEPN聚合多个不相交支持回合生成的原型，以其均值作为最终类代表；在DeFACTO传感器数据集上构建五类故障分类任务，注入合成偏置、漂移、尖峰与噪声故障；每回合单样本(K=1)设置下聚合N_agg=10个支持回合，并与K=10的ProtoNet在相同10样本预算下对比。

Result: 100次独立运行中，MEPN在单回合单样本(K=1, N_agg=10)设置下达到文中给出的准确率，明显高于单回合基线；在等量10样本预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 原型积累是性能提升的机制而非固定预算学习的优势；多回合聚合可在低标注条件下稳定原型，为少样本工业故障诊断提供简单有效方案。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [159] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出腕部IMU到全身质心加速度的简化运动学模型(KM)及三类混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)，结果证明腕部IMU可估计质心加速度，混合模型精度与噪声鲁棒性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部IMU广泛用于日常健康监测，但无法完全反映全身动力学，而身体质心(COM)被视为生理参考标准。因此需要建立从腕部IMU到质心加速度的映射方法。

Method: 提出基于若干简化假设的简化运动学模型(KM)，使仅凭腕部IMU测量即可求解动力学方程；并进一步提出三类混合AI建模方法HKM-NN，包括串行学习(ser-)和两种同步学习方式(sim1-和sim2-)，结合灰箱与黑箱建模优势。使用10名健康志愿者在六种步态活动和坐-站过渡动作中的数据训练与测试。

Result: KM模型在步态活动中误差为6.7%~12.5%，坐-站为5.6%；HKM-NN模型显著提升性能，步态活动误差为5.3%~9.3%，坐-站最佳误差为3.9%。在噪声测试条件下，sim1-/sim2-对高斯扰动鲁棒性更强，而KM模型对椒盐噪声鲁棒性较强。

Conclusion: 研究证明从腕部IMU估计质心加速度可行，凸显了将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合对于不完美、含噪测量条件下可穿戴传感应用的重要性。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [160] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新评估Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL)在机器遗忘中的三种角色，审计公开实现发现两处正确性问题，修正后DPL在直接删除任务上全部失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究将DPL定位为机器遗忘的三种角色：直接删除信号（最强主张）、保效正则化器和对抗式遗忘的热启动。但较弱角色的证据被用来支持最强主张，缺乏在匹配协议下对各角色的独立检验。

Method: 在匹配协议下，以精确种子重训练为基线，分别测试DPL三种角色。审计公开实现，发现两个正确性问题：图像方向在增强归一化张量上计算却应用于原始图像；标签扰动低于float32分辨率导致标签未改变。修正图像扰动流程后进行验证。

Result: 修正后，DPL在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18的三个配对种子上均未通过直接删除标准；其效用影响跨种子符号不一致，且计入方向计算时间后不如简单热启动基线。单种子Tiny ImageNet检验同样不支持DPL作正则化器或热启动。

Conclusion: 结果仅覆盖随机实例删除，不排除其他删除机制下的影响函数方法。作者发布角色匹配的评估协议和针对基于扰动删除主张的审计清单。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [161] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一个支持多智能体、多用户的隐私感知统一记忆框架，通过动态区分私有/公共信息并实施索引级访问控制，实现跨用户共享知识；同时发布首个多用户记忆基准MUMBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM仅限单个用户会话，知识局限于单一对话，无法学习随时间演变的用户偏好；现有智能体记忆系统虽有所改进，但多在单用户层面运作，限制了可跨用户共享、以提升下游响应的公共知识。

Method: AIM框架动态将信息分类为私有（仅限单一用户访问）或公共（所有用户可访问），并实施索引级访问控制，使私有记忆仅能被其所有者检索，从而在保护敏感数据的同时允许共享知识提升协作与一致性。同时构建MUMBench，涵盖四个领域的多用户交互数据，包含私有与可共享信息，支持检索、创建、更新和删除等记忆操作的评估。

Result: 在MUMBench上进行三次独立运行，AIM取得96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率和70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM为多用户、多智能体LLM系统提供了隐私感知的持久记忆管理方案，兼顾隐私保护与知识共享；MUMBench是首个面向多用户环境、评估多种记忆操作的公开数据集，为该方向提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [162] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Affective Agent——一个面向可穿戴设备的三层（感知、个性化、推理）参考架构，在本地整合紧凑语言模型与生理证据、上下文和用户历史，实现不确定条件下的个性化干预推理，无需云端依赖或逐用户重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算在可穿戴状态推断方面已有进展，但设备端难以推理"是否、何时、如何"进行干预，且需解决不确定性、个性化与算力受限等问题。

Method: 结合亚十亿参数的小型语言模型与生理证据、上下文、用户历史；通过主机管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，而非逐用户权重更新；由三层架构（感知、个性化、推理）交互；在室内环境质量控制场景中实例化，并用留出的模拟器生成纵向场景进行评估。

Result: 在合成评估中，记忆驱动的个性化与两遍结构化推理改进了干预决策。

Conclusion: 将决策层移至设备端，为从可穿戴状态推断走向可穿戴级硬件上的闭环个性化干预提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [163] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LoRA-RC，用低秩修正在线自适应储备池循环矩阵，并投影到谱范数球以保证收缩与稳定性；在Lorenz漂移系统上显著降低漂移后预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备计算（RC）仅训练线性读出，快速且数据高效，适合在线预测；但静态储备池在系统漂移下性能退化，仅自适应读出不足，而无约束的储备池自适应又会破坏回声状态与增量稳定性，因此需要一种受约束的在线储备池自适应方法。

Method: LoRA-RC通过由流式预测误差驱动的低秩修正来调整循环矩阵；基础储备池与自适应基离线固定，仅一个小的核心矩阵在线自适应，每步投影到谱范数球并进行低通滤波。

Result: 投影保证每个实际应用的循环矩阵都位于经认证的收缩集内，并沿每条在线自适应路径建立了增量输入到状态稳定性界，其速率与增益与路径无关。在发生突变参数漂移的Lorenz系统上，LoRA-RC相比固定RC将漂移后预测误差降低56%，相比仅读出自适应降低51%；20个随机种子的消融实验显示，移除投影会使该误差膨胀40倍以上。

Conclusion: 受约束的低秩在线自适应可在保持RC稳定性的同时有效应对系统漂移，投影约束对性能与稳定性至关重要。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [164] [Harness or Model? Isolating the Harness Effect in Agentic Coding with a Contamination-Controlled Private Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11987)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在256项私有防污染任务套件上做同模型配对实验，比较厂商原生harness与中立harness（deepagents）。两组对比均未发现harness的平均优势：Opus 4.8为-1.25pp，GPT-5.5为+1.25pp；但Opus的平均值掩盖了分层反向效应，且成本比较因用量记录缺失而无法定论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设厂商原生harness与其自家模型配对能解决更多任务，但这一假设缺乏严格的实证检验。本文旨在用同模型配对对照，量化harness选择对编码智能体任务成功率与成本的影响。

Method: 在私有、污染可控的256项任务套件（含仓库任务与截止日期后竞赛题）上做配对同模型对比：80项任务分别在claude-opus-4-8上跑claude-agent-sdk与deepagents、在gpt-5.5上跑openai-codex SDK与deepagents，另以gemini-3.5-flash和deepseek-v3.2作旁支单元格。计划800次运行中792次由隔离oracle评分；成本按原始逐轮用量与固定价目表重新计价。

Result: 两种对比均未确认任一方harness的平均优势：Opus 4.8为-1.25pp（48.8% vs 50.0%，任务自助法95% CI [-10.0, +7.5]），GPT-5.5为+1.25pp（55.6% vs 54.4%，CI [-4.4, +6.9]）。Opus平均值由相反分层构成：原生harness在61项仓库任务落后9.0pp，却在19项竞赛任务领先23.7pp（标签置换 p=0.003），但该划分系事后选定，需设计性重复。正确性与完成度亦分离：81次撞墙钟上限被取消的运行中有22次已产出可通过的补丁。按固定价目重算，中立harness每解决一任务的成本在Opus 4.8上高1.3–1.6倍、GPT-5.5上高1.2倍；Anthropic账户有58次运行无用量记录，将其支出分配到任一方可使Opus比值在0.7–2.3之间移动，故计费排序未定。本修订纠正了2026年8月稿因自身遥测用量语义缺陷导致的成本数字。

Conclusion: 厂商原生harness并不带来稳定的平均性能优势，harness效应可能高度依赖任务类型；成本比较受遥测数据缺陷制约，尚无定论。作者公开编排器、评分oracle、重分析代码与派生聚合数据，任务本身保持私有。

Abstract: An agentic coding system couples a language model to a harness: the tools, prompts and control flow that turn a chat model into an autonomous software engineer. Vendors ship harnesses tuned to their own models, and practitioners assume the vendor-native pairing solves more tasks. We measure that assumption with paired same-model contrasts on a private, contamination-controlled suite of 256 repository and post-cutoff contest tasks. The same 80 tasks ran under claude-agent-sdk and under deepagents on claude-opus-4-8, and under the openai-codex SDK and deepagents on gpt-5.5, with gemini-3.5-flash and deepseek-v3.2 as side cells. 792 of 800 planned runs were graded by an isolated oracle. Neither contrast resolves an average advantage for either harness: -1.25 pp for Opus 4.8 (48.8% vs 50.0%, task-bootstrap 95% CI [-10.0, +7.5]) and +1.25 pp for GPT-5.5 (55.6% vs 54.4%, CI [-4.4, +6.9]). The Opus average combines opposite strata: the native harness trails by 9.0 pp on the 61 repository tasks and leads by 23.7 pp on the 19 contest tasks (label-permutation p = 0.003). The partition was chosen after seeing the data and needs a designed replication. Correctness and completion also separate: 22 of 81 runs cancelled at the wall-clock ceiling had produced a passing patch. Re-priced from raw per-turn usage at frozen list prices, the neutral harness cost 1.3 to 1.6 times as much per solved task on Opus 4.8 and 1.2 times on GPT-5.5. These are observed-usage estimates. On the Anthropic account 58 runs left no usage record, and allocating that spend to either cell would move the Opus ratio between 0.7 and 2.3, so the billed ordering is unresolved. This revision corrects an August 2026 manuscript whose cost figures rested on a usage-semantics defect in our own telemetry (Section 5.1). We release the orchestrator, grading oracle, reanalysis code and derived aggregates. The tasks stay private.

</details>


### [165] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CORE框架，通过不确定性感知的信念修正，将回合局部证据与持久人格状态更新分离，选择性地更新用户偏好，并构建PERSIST基准评测人格状态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言模型面临"人格漂移"问题：用户画像随长期交互不断演变而并非固定不变。模型需在偏好真实改变时修正持久人格状态，同时避免因短暂、模糊或未解决的观察而错误更新。

Method: 提出CORE框架，将回合局部证据与持久人格状态修正相分离，通过不确定性感知的信念修正机制，选择性更新有依据的用户偏好。同时构建PERSIST基准，用于在序列交互压力下（涵盖模糊性、冲突与受控社会影响）评测人格状态的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ALOE、PersonaChat和PERSIST上，CORE提升了个性化对齐与鲁棒性，并在归一化封闭槽位状态保真度上取得互补增益。人工评估与机制性对照进一步表明，其显式更新控制优于仅依赖更强的生成能力或持久记忆。

Conclusion: 显式的、基于不确定性的更新控制对于维护持久人格状态至关重要，是单纯增强生成或记忆能力所无法替代的，为长期个性化交互提供了稳健方案。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [166] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueLM-GUI，一个35B-A3B的移动端GUI智能体，以真机为中心的飞轮方法论，通过"Every Sample Matters、Every Rollout Is Real、Every Query Evolves"三条原则，在MobileGUI-VBench上达87.4分，AndroidWorld上达84.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体正从多模块框架转向端到端原生模型训练，但工业部署面临三大持续存在的鸿沟：（1）沙盒训练与生产环境存在分布不匹配；（2）昂贵真机失败样本未被充分利用；（3）固定基准趋于饱和，丧失指导迭代的能力。

Method: 构建三大原则组成的真机中心飞轮：Every Sample Matters——双轨流水线，含异构三系统共识评估与错误纠正及派生模块，将每条轨迹转化为可用监督；Every Rollout Is Real——三阶段配方（持续预训练、监督微调、真实手机上的智能体强化学习），使每次rollout扎根真实生产环境；Every Query Evolves——配额驱动的基准方法，含三个正交维度，实现精确归因并支持基准随模型提升而系统升级。

Result: BlueLM-GUI在MobileGUI-VBench上达87.4分，超过最佳闭源模型5.1分；在AndroidWorld上达84.9分，为开源模型最佳结果，并与闭源模型具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将模型训练与迭代改进扎根于真实设备及三条Every原则，能够产出强健、鲁棒且可迁移的移动GUI能力。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [167] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对3DGS中"神经化"趋势进行梳理，沿五个维度分类并分析19种代表性方法，指出神经参数化并非二元选择，应选择性使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS结合显式图元与高效光栅化，但近期系统越来越多地用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数。作者希望厘清这一趋势，判断"神经化"是否真的有效，以及不同神经化方式各自解决什么问题。

Method: 沿五个维度刻画神经参数化趋势：属性解码、空间共享、视角条件解码、拓扑生成、摊销推理。分析19种代表性方法。并在受控的mip-NeRF 360实验中分离出三种神经参数化形式进行对比验证。

Result: 对19种方法的分析表明这些选择针对不同局限，不能简化为"是否神经化"的二元标签。受控实验显示：共享外观与不透明度能提升重建质量，而解码几何结构没有带来额外收益。

Conclusion: 证据支持"选择性神经化"：当共享函数能捕获可复用的相关性、且不牺牲显式splat的局部几何自由度时，它才是有益的。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [168] [Confidence-Gated Transductive Test Generation for Code Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12489)
*Sungjae Lee,Youngsik Yoon,Seockbean Song,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoTT（置信度门控转导测试生成）方法，通过仅在归纳置信度低时调用转导生成，实现测试用例合成的效率与效果平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试用例合成对评估与排序大语言模型生成的程序至关重要，但构建高质量测试用例困难，因为可靠的期望输出往往难以获得。现有方法（如转导生成）虽然可靠但计算成本高。

Method: CoTT先采用高效的归纳过程，仅当归纳置信度较低时才触发转导生成。这种自适应设计按需分配额外计算资源。

Result: 在代码重排序基准上，CoTT在所报告指标上优于已有基线，同时相比对每个输入都使用转导生成，降低了成本。

Conclusion: 基于置信度的测试时计算分配，在使用单个高效LLM的情况下，实现了有利的效率—效果权衡。

Abstract: Test case synthesis is crucial for evaluating and ranking programs generated by large language models (LLMs). However, constructing high-quality test cases remains challenging because reliable expected outputs are often difficult to obtain. We propose Confidence-Gated Transductive Test Generation (CoTT), which first uses an efficient inductive procedure and invokes transductive generation only when inductive confidence is low. This adaptive design improves output reliability while allocating extra computation only when needed. On code reranking benchmarks, CoTT outperforms prior baselines across the reported metrics while reducing cost relative to applying transductive generation to every input. These results show that confidence-based allocation of test-time computation provides a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off with a single efficient LLM.

</details>


### [169] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了 Core 协同演化算法，通过将智能体生态位划分到环境的特定子区域，验证其在局部子环境中的适应能力优于整体环境和不同子环境的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Core 是一种不依赖传统适应度函数的竞争性协同演化算法，此前已证明能在 Xpilot 游戏中演化出具备战斗和导航能力的智能体。作者希望进一步探究将智能体生态位划分到原始环境的特定子集后，是否能提升其局部适应优势。

Method: 在 Core 算法框架下基于局部交互（锦标赛选择、交叉和变异）产生后代并演化控制器，将智能体进行生态位划分（niching），分别针对原始环境的特定子集进行演化，并与针对整个系统演化及针对不同子环境演化的智能体进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，针对特定子环境进行生态位划分的智能体在与针对整个系统演化的智能体以及针对不同子环境演化的智能体的竞争中，表现出更强的成功能力。

Conclusion: 生态位划分到特定子环境能够提升 Core 算法演化智能体的局部竞争表现，验证了局部化演化在协同演化控制中的有效性，为无适应度函数条件下的自主控制演化提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [170] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA：面向生成式推荐大束解码的线性注意力框架，通过共享提示状态与追加式分歧记录，避免为每个束重建完整循环状态，实现1.54-2.46倍端到端解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖大束解码生成数百个候选项，给循环线性注意力带来新的扩展挑战。现有线性注意力服务系统要么为每个束实体化完整循环状态，要么重复回放共享历史，造成显著的内存与数据传输开销。

Method: OneLA利用GR工作负载共享提示词、仅后缀分歧的特点：用单个共享的提示派生状态加紧凑的仅追加分歧转移记录表示所有束状态；每步解码仅计算所需状态信息，不重建每个束的完整循环状态；使用轻量级祖先索引追踪构成各束历史的转移记录，使束更新无需移动或复制已有记录；并采用融合GPU内核跨束复用共享状态。

Result: 端到端解码速度提升1.54-2.46倍，同时大幅降低循环状态内存占用与数据移动。

Conclusion: 通过利用共享提示与短分歧后缀的结构特性，OneLA有效缓解了生成式推荐大束解码中线性注意力的内存与带宽瓶颈，兼顾效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [171] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化进化算法，独立进化六足机器人每条腿的步态，无需集中协调即可涌现高效稳定的运动行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统六足机器人步态优化依赖集中式协调，复杂度高，难以实现可扩展和自适应的运动控制。

Method: 使用Webots仿真器和Mantis六足机器人，通过去中心化进化算法独立优化每条腿的控制器，避免集中协调，让涌现行为驱动协调运动，并与协同协同进化方法进行对比基准测试。

Result: 去中心化方法在生成稳定、自适应步态方面优于协同协同进化，并展现出有趣的涌现协调现象。

Conclusion: 独立进化腿部控制器降低了步态优化复杂度，凸显了去中心化策略在可扩展、自适应机器人系统中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [172] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过程感知的语言认知诊断框架PLCD，用语言结构作为认知先验、用作答记录校准学生后验状态，在预测性能与认知迁移上优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型依赖离散的、基于ID的嵌入来表示学生、习题和概念，这与学习者认知中知识并非以孤立符号存储和提取的本质不符，因此在出现新习题或新概念时存在语义局限（冷启动问题）。

Method: PLCD利用大语言模型构建概念图式和认知过程图；使用目标条件语义记忆检索与每个目标习题相关的历史作答；通过带DA-MoE专家和过程级对比学习的过程锚定Language-to-Cognition Mapper，将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。

Result: 实验结果表明，PLCD在学生表现预测上优于传统基线，并展现出较强的认知迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将LLM的计算能力与测量潜在知识状态的心理测量目标相连接，表明由作答记录校准的结构化语言先验可提升冷启动鲁棒性和认知接地性。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [173] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VRL-Bench评测框架与VEX²调度器，在有限试错预算下公平评估语言智能体的言语记忆学习，VEX²在全部六个设置中均实现正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 言语强化学习（如Reflexion）能将失败试错转为文本以指导后续尝试，但现有评估缺乏统一的试错预算，导致方法间比较不公平，且各方法效果不稳定。

Method: 构建VRL-Bench评测框架，在MiniWoB与WebShop上、三种模型条件下，对Reflexion及后续多种言语记忆方法的更新进行有限预算下的公平评估；并通过重放实验分析反思机制；进而提出VEX²——一种利用语言模型联合选择策略与分配剩余试错预算的探索-利用调度器。

Result: 各言语记忆方法在部分设置中优于无记忆重试，但在另一些设置中反而更差；重放实验显示反思可能降低成功率，揭示利用经验与持续探索之间的权衡；VEX²是唯一在全部六个设置中相对重试均取得正向成功率增益的更新方法。

Conclusion: 试错学习方法的收益依赖于预算与设置，单纯依赖反思存在风险；将探索-利用权衡显式调度（VEX²）能更稳定地提升语言智能体的试错学习表现。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets.
